# Sales Performance Executive Summary...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:32, y:57]**
Sales Performance Executive Summary

- The REDMAGIC 11 Air achieved significant initial success across all channels, reaching $2.04M in DTC sales during its first month. This represents a 130% increase in sales volume compared to the REDMAGIC 10 Air launch. Mercado Libre increased 153%; Offline channels 27.5%.

- Sales forecast of the first week is 98.73% accurate. 

- During the teasing phase, overall awareness for the 11 Air increased by 16%. But during the Launch to Open Sales stages, the traffic lagged behind the 10 Air. The traffic gap mainly comes from ads.

# Sales Review - Mercado Libre  2026.02.10 - 2026.03...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:40, y:45]**
Sales Review - Mercado Libre  2026.02.10 - 2026.03.11

**[position: x:47, y:93]**
| Models | 11 Air | 10 Air | Compare |
|---|---|---|---|
| 12GB+256GB | 518 | 413 | 125%↑ |
| 16GB+512GB | 316 | 130 | 243%↑ |
| Total | 834 | 543 | 153%↑ |

**[position: x:47, y:200]**
|  | Clicks | Units Sold/pcs | CVR | Revenue |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Mercado Libre | 145,101 | 834 | 0.57% | $581,880 |
| DTC (Mexico) | 53,028 | 364 | 0.31% | $238,636 |

**[position: x:44, y:292]**
11 Air 实现了超过 10 万次点击，销量达到了预期的 111%。整体转化率为 0.57%，处于该价格区间的正常水平。但考虑到新品上线后的流量获取投入，整体转化率略高于预期。
此外，11 Air 的用户售后反馈相比上一代 10 Air 有显著提升。用户满意度（尤其是在 REDMAGIC 全功能配置和设计方面）已超过 90%。仅有少部分用户对发热和相机提出了负面反馈。
同时，随着 11 Air 的发布，品牌店铺新增用户约 2000 人。
11 Air achieved over 100K clicks, and unit sales reached 111% of expectations. The overall CVR was 0.57%, which falls within the normal range for its price segment. However, considering the traffic acquisition investment following the new product launch, the overall conversion rate is slightly higher than expected.

Additionally, user after-sales feedback for the 11 Air has improved significantly compared to the previous-generation 10 Air. Customer satisfaction for the 11 Air—particularly regarding its full-featured REDMAGIC configuration and design—has reached over 90%. Only a small portion of users have expressed negative feedback related to heat and the camera. Furthermore, with the launch of the 11 Air, the brand store gained approximately 2,000 new users

**[position: x:423, y:101]**
| 区域 | 10 Air出货 （台） | 11 Air出货 （台） | Vs 10 Air |
|---|---|---|---|
| SEA | 301 | 518 | 72.1% |
| ME (含MEA BU) | 118 | 160 | 35.6% |
| HK | 82 | 56 | -31.7% |
| CIS | 90 | 140 | 55.6% |
| EU | 10 | 52 | 420.0% |

**[position: x:40, y:295]**
客户类型：零售商 / 贸易商 / 分销商 Customers type: Retailers/Traders/Distributors
销售数量基于实际售出数量（sell-through） Sales Qty is based on sell-through quantity

**[position: x:36, y:344]**
11 Air整体销售态势较10 Air有了较好的增长，市场表现稳步向上，首销月如能有更多的供应，预期销量会更多；
256GB 黑色 依然是出货量的中流砥柱（337台，同比增20%）；同时，因高配提货价下调，512GB版本也实现跨越式翻倍增长，11 Air高低配的需求量的差异显著缩小；
SEA是销售主力，不仅在全局中占比高达54%，且较前代大幅增长72.1%；此外，CIS和ME市场也分别保持了55.5%和35.5%的增幅，EU销量也有了比较好的增长。

Overall sales performance of the 11 Air has shown strong growth compared to the 10 Air, with steady upward market momentum. If more supply had been available during the first month of sales, total sales would have likely been even higher.
The 256GB Black variant remains the core volume driver (337 units, +20% YoY). At the same time, due to a reduction in the wholesale price of higher configurations, the 512GB version achieved significant growth, more than doubling. As a result, the demand gap between lower and higher configurations has narrowed considerably.
Southeast Asia (SEA) remains the primary sales driver, accounting for 54% of total sales and achieving a strong 72.1% increase compared to the previous generation. In addition, the CIS and Middle East (ME) markets recorded growth of 55.5% and 35.5%, respectively, while Europe (EU) also saw solid sales growth.

Due to limited supply of the 11 Air, distribution in the first month was heavily prioritized toward the Singapore, Malaysia, and Thailand markets. As a result, SEA recorded a 72.1% increase in first-month sales compared to the 10 Air, making it the primary driver of overall growth.

得益于泰国市场的开拓，以及三方的密切配合，泰国市场整体发展速度较快。
Thanks to the expansion into the Thailand market, along with close collaboration among all parties, the Thailand market has developed rapidly.

# DTC Attachment Rate...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:138, y:-1]**
DTC Attachment Rate

**[position: x:7, y:354]**
- 整体壳膜的附着情况比10Air健康，壳与膜附着情况近似，10Air的膜明显高于壳较多。

- 保护膜：与10Air附着情况差不多，略低于10Air

- 保护壳：附着情况各站点都提升50%-100%，与膜差不多，原因是11Air卖的是普通的壳，价格较10 Air散热壳便宜，低客单价拉高了转化率，满足Air 用户“既想保护手机，又不愿支付高额功能溢价”的心理


- 整体生态配件上，与11Pro相比，呈现“入门级”特征。

- 散热器的选择上，11Air用户选择Cooler 8 Air和6 Air轻量款的占比相对11Pro的多，对极端散热性能追求不高，更看重配件的便携性和价格

- 手柄等外设消费力也较旗舰机型的弱，与10Air近似。

# DTC Attachment Rate...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:138, y:-1]**
DTC Attachment Rate

**[position: x:22, y:270]**
Overall, the attachment rate for the 11 Pro continues to center on "essential" accessories like cases and screen protectors, supplemented by other gaming peripherals.
Screen Protectors & Cases: Compared to the 10 Pro, the attachment rate for screen protectors saw exponential growth, mirroring the hardware sales. For phone cases, the strategy shifted from offering a choice between "Cooling" and "Standard" cases to a single standard model. While the attachment rate for this single case is slightly lower than the combined total of the two 10 Pro case types, the conversion rate for the single SKU improved, simplifying the consumer decision-making process.
Gamepads: Aside from coolers, gamepads are the most popular accessory, with a higher attachment rate than the previous generation. Beyond the iterative hardware improvements, this can be attributed to the Gamepad 3's higher price point, which created a psychological "flagship phone deserves a flagship controller" appeal. Combined with launch bundle discounts, this significantly boosted purchase intent.
Coolers: The attachment rate for coolers is lower than that of the 10 Pro. This is likely because the 11 Pro's upgraded internal cooling technology (liquid cooling system) has internalized some of the heat management needs, reducing the perceived necessity for an external cooler.
Regional Insights: In the Middle East, the attachment rate for gaming peripherals is consistently above average, demonstrating strong market potential. Moving forward, we should consider more customized peripheral + console bundles for this region.


=====

1. 用户对高配（16+512 GB）的接受度提升明显，尤其是相比旧型号 10 Air， 占比接近40% ， 高配需求仍需重视。

2. 低配（12+256 GB）仍是主流，但比 10 Air 略低，可能意味着用户升级意愿上升。

3. 黑色依旧是用户首选的颜色。无论高低配，黑色销量都远高于白色，显示颜色影响购买决策。

- 11 Air 表现远强于 10 Air： 无论是首日爆发力还是后续的每日销量，11 Air 都占据绝对优势。首日销量差距： 11 Air 首日销量高达 1,039，而 10 Air 仅为 313，增长接近3.3 倍 左右，说明 11 Air 的市场预期和预热效果更好，也受到机制调整的影响。

- 约在第 3 天之后，两者的下降坡度减缓，进入了相对稳定的长尾销售期。在第21天左右巴西上线，都获得一个小高峰。

- 竞争差距收窄： 在销售后期（第 40 天后），两者的差距虽依然存在，但 10 Air 有几次接近 11 Air 的表现（如第 42 天、第 51 天），说明 11 Air 的领先优势在随时间缓慢收缩。

12+256 GB: 61%, vs 10 Air -20%16+512 GB: 39%, vs 10 Air +63%


- User acceptance of the high-end configuration (16+512 GB) has increased significantly, especially compared to the older 10 Air model, accounting for nearly 40%. Demand for the high-end version still requires attention.

- The low-end configuration (12+256 GB) remains the mainstream choice, but slightly lower than 10 Air, which may indicate an increased willingness among users to upgrade.

- Black remains the preferred color for users. Regardless of configuration, black sales far exceed white, showing that color influences purchasing decisions.

**[position: x:43, y:276]**
Traffic
3.37M (All Store. From Shopify)
↗66%

**[position: x:43, y:347]**
CVR
0.32% (All Store. From Shopify)
↗8%

**[position: x:410, y:386]**
In terms of both first-day sales momentum and subsequent baseline sales, the 11 Air holds an absolute advantage. On the first day, 11 Air sold 1,039 units, compared to only 313 units for 10 Air. The former is roughly 3.3 times the latter, indicating stronger market expectations and more effective pre-launch hype for 11 Air.

After around the third day, the decline in sales for both models slowed, entering a relatively stable long-tail sales period. Around day 21, when launched in Brazil, both experienced a small peak.

Competition Gap Narrowing:
In the later sales period (after day 40), the gap between the two models still exists, but 10 Air occasionally approached 11 Air’s performance (e.g., day 42, day 51), indicating that 11 Air’s lead is gradually narrowing over time.

站点销售比例：

- 第一梯队 (巨量市场)： EU 和US这两个站点是品牌的绝对重心。

- 第二梯队 (稳定市场)： Mexico 两款11 Air和10 Air机型都有稳定的 10% 占比。

- 第三梯队 (长尾市场)： CA, Global, UK, Brazil。销量占比均在 4%-7% 之间徘徊。
各个站带全站流量和转化率情况：

- 流量上除了墨西哥，大部分地区都是全站流量对比上一代都有增长。

- 全站转化率中，欧洲英国加拿大新加坡和中东都有下降。
Sales Mix by Region
Tier 1 (Core Markets): EU and US remain the brand’s primary growth drivers, contributing the largest share of sales.
Tier 2 (Stable Market): Mexico maintains a consistent ~10% share across both models.
Tier 3 (Long-tail Markets): CA, Global, UK, and Brazil each contribute around 4%–7% of total sales.
Traffic & Conversion Performance
Across most regions, overall site traffic increased vs. the previous generation, with Mexico being the main exception.

# Sales Review - DTC Channel 2026.02.10 - 2026.03.11...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:40, y:45]**
Sales Review - DTC Channel 2026.02.10 - 2026.03.11 Open Sales in 30 Days

**[position: x:479, y:97]**
1. 页面浏览量PV 对比：

- 爆发点：墨西哥 (MX)

  - 数据： 11 Air 的 PV 达到 133,543，比 10 Air (42,100) 增长了 217%。

  - 洞察： 11 Air 在墨西哥市场引发了巨大的关注，市场热度远超前代。

- 主要下滑区：NA站，Global站和新加坡站

  - 数据： 北美下滑 31%，GL站下滑 29%，新加坡下滑 47%。

  - GL站：和10 Air相比, 印度流量-95%， 哥伦比亚-89%，菲律宾-91%，智利-71%。 但是转化率和整体订单量都比10Air期间增长100%+（尤其是澳大利亚+336%，但瑞士相对平稳），说明流量更加精准。

  - 新加坡：主要来自Search的流量减少
2. 转化率 (CR) 对比：

- 转化效率高：墨西哥 (MX) 与 北美 (NA)

  - 数据： 墨西哥 CR 从 0.02% 暴涨至 0.31% (+1450%)；北美从 0.23% 提升至 0.44% (+91%)。

  - 洞察： 虽然在北美流量变少了，但购买意愿却大幅提升。说明 11 Air 的产品定位精准击中了这些玩家的痛点，或者由于流量更加精准，转化效率极高。

- 转化率增长高：阿联酋 (UAE) 和GL站点

  - 数据： CR 达到 1.10%（除去pending orders后CRV为0.26%, 10 Air 仅 0.13%）

  - GL站点：流量更加精确，可售国家转化率增长500%.

- 转化下降国家：英国和MEA站点

  - 英国：英国站点整体流量，订阅率都相对减少。

  - MEA站：主要是沙特阿拉伯国家的流量和转化率下降

**[position: x:490, y:94]**
1. Pageviews (PV) Comparison
Breakout Market: Mexico (MX)

- Data: 11 Air PV reached 133,543, vs. 10 Air (42,100), a +217% increase

- Insight: Strong surge in market interest, significantly outperforming the previous generation
Declining Markets: NA, Global (GL), Singapore (SG)

- Data: NA -31%, GL -29%, SG -47%

- GL Details: Traffic declined sharply vs. 10 Air (India -95%, Colombia -89%, Philippines -91%, Chile -71%) However, conversion rate and total orders increased by 100%+ (notably Australia +336%, Switzerland stable), indicating more targeted traffic

- Singapore: Decline mainly driven by reduced search traffic

2. Conversion Rate (CR) Comparison
High Conversion Efficiency: Mexico (MX) & North America (NA)

- Data:

  - MX: 0.02% → 0.31% (+1450%)

  - NA: 0.23% → 0.44% (+91%)

- Insight: Despite lower traffic in NA, purchase intent significantly increased, suggesting stronger product-market fit and/or more qualified traffic
Strong CR Growth: UAE & GL

- UAE Data: CR reached 1.10% (CRV 0.26% excluding pending orders, vs. 10 Air 0.13%)

- GL Insight: More precise traffic led to +500% CR growth in sellable countries
Declining Conversion Markets: UK & MEA

- UK: Decline in both traffic and subscription rate

- MEA: Mainly driven by decreased traffic and CR in Saudi Arabia

# Sales Review - DTC Channel  开售后国家销售占比变化...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
表现突出的国家/地区：

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:40, y:45]**
Sales Review - DTC Channel  开售后国家销售占比变化

**[position: x:791, y:84]**
Source: metabase

**[position: x:545, y:99]**
开售后各个主要国家（份额在1%以上）销售比例变化：
1.核心市场迁移：美国市场成为绝对核心

- 11 Air销量占比最高的国家为：美国，墨西哥，法国，德国，英国，加拿大，西班牙。10 Air最高国家为：美国，法国，墨西哥，英国，加拿大，德国。

- 美国（US）市场爆发： 11 Air 在美国的销售份额从 10 Air 的 23.5% 飙升至 32.6%（份额增长约 +9.1%）。美国依然是成为全球最重要的单一市场。

- 墨西哥（MX）市场稳健： 墨西哥其销售份额保持在 9.8% 左右，显示出极在次旗舰机型中其稳定的用户忠诚度和市场地位
2. 主要下滑区域：英加欧市场权重降低

- 英国（GB）与加拿大（CA）份额缩减: 英国的销售占比从 8.6% 下滑至 5.1%。

- 加拿大的销售占比从 7.2% 下滑至 4.3%。

- 洞察： 这种在核心英语系国家（除美国外）的份额下滑，可能意味着竞品在当地的攻势加强，或 11 Air 在当地市场的竞争力相比前代有所波动。

- 欧洲（法国 FR、西班牙 ES、荷兰 NL、葡萄牙 和 比利时）同步下滑： 法国和西班牙等传统欧洲市场的占比均有 1%~2% 的降幅。这表明 11 Air 的增长主要依靠美国，且其他新兴市场增长，而欧洲市场的相对贡献在减弱。
3. 新兴市场增长：拉美与中东显示潜力

- 巴西（BR）与哥伦比亚（CO）： 巴西的市场占比从 2.1% 提升至 3.0%；新增可售地区哥伦比亚更是异军突起（份额增长 +0.8%，销量1→38）。

- 沙特阿拉伯（SA）： 作为中东核心，沙特的占比从 1.9% 提升至 2.7%。洞察： 品牌在拉美和中东地区的下沉与渗透在 11 Air 这一代取得了成效，这些新兴市场正在成为品牌新的增长点。

**[position: x:13, y:493]**
Source: Shopify

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:40, y:45]**
Sales Review - DTC Channel  Post-launch Sales Share by Country (Change)

**[position: x:791, y:84]**
Source: metabase

**[position: x:545, y:99]**
Sales Mix Shift by Country (Post-Launch, Markets >1% Share)
1. Core Market Shift: US Still the Primary Driver

- Top markets for 11 Air: US, Mexico, France, Germany, UK, Canada, Spain(10 Air: US, France, Mexico, UK, Canada, Germany)

- US Surge: Sales share increased from 23.5% → 32.6% (+9.1pp), reinforcing the US as the most critical single market globally.

- Mexico Stability: Share remains stable at ~9.8%, indicating strong user loyalty and consistent performance in the sub-flagship segment.
2. Declining Regions: UK, Canada & Europe

- UK: 8.6% → 5.1%

- Canada: 7.2% → 4.3%

- Insight: The decline across key English-speaking markets (ex-US) may suggest stronger competitive pressure or relatively weaker positioning of 11 Air vs. its predecessor.

- EU (FR, ES, NL, PT, BE): Most markets declined by ~1–2pp
Insight: Growth is increasingly driven by the US and emerging markets, while Europe’s relative contribution is weakening. 
3. Emerging Market Growth: LATAM & Middle East

- Brazil (BR): 2.1% → 3.0%

- Colombia (CO): Newly launched market, share +0.8pp (units: 1 → 38)

- Saudi Arabia (SA): 1.9% → 2.7%

- Insight: Expansion and penetration in LATAM and the Middle East are gaining traction, making these regions new growth drivers for 11 Air.

**[position: x:13, y:493]**
Source: Shopify

| INTL Store: | 7,616 | 1% | 40,744 | 7% | 96,976 | -22% | 242% |

**[position: x:11, y:297]**
Teasing 阶段，11 Air 的整体声量增长16%, 在各个商城也是上涨趋势. 
Launch + EBO阶段, 以及开售后30天阶段, 11 Air流量整体低于10 Air, 跳出率 / 停留时长 / 用户浏览页面数表现也都略逊色于10 Air. 
销量增长, 由于每个商城的转化率上升.

EU和BR Store, 开售后EU和BR流量曲线维持的好, 流量衰退慢. 
与11 Pro相比, 各个商城11 Air大约占其30-50%的流量. BR和MX商城的用户, 对11 Air的兴趣度最高, 基本与11 Pro持平.

During the teasing phase, overall awareness for the 11 Air increased by 16%.
During the launch + EBO phase and the first 30 days after sales began, overall traffic for the 11 Air was lower than that of the 10 Air. Engagement metrics—including bounce rate, time on site, and pages per user—also performed slightly weaker compared to the 10 Air.
Despite this, sales increased, driven by higher conversion rates across each store.
For the EU and BR stores, post-launch traffic remained relatively stable, with a slower decline compared to other regions.
Compared to the 11 Pro, traffic for the 11 Air accounts for approximately 30–50% across different stores. Notably, users in the BR and MX stores show the highest interest in the 11 Air, with traffic levels nearly on par with the 11 Pro.

# Sales Review - DTC Channel Traffic Analysis Source...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:136, y:11]**
Sales Review - DTC Channel Traffic Analysis Source: ConvBox

**[position: x:25, y:57]**
各个商城流量结构不同, 从流量渠道分析流量变化, 大致分为三大类:


- INTL 商城, 流量减少几乎全部来自广告, 广告占比58%降到41%. UK 商城和CA 商城也是类似情况


- MX商城, 流量减少来自全渠道的降低, 广告降低最多, 其次是Organic和YouTube.


- EU商城, 流量增加主要来自广告和红人增长
SA商城, 流量增加主要来自红人
BR商城, 流量增长由KOL带动, KOL和Organic同步增长, 同时广告流量增长高. 

各个商城流量结构健康, 广告流量占比40-50%. 其中巴西商城广告流量最低14%, 红人驱动流量增长明显.

Air次旗舰系列产品和Pro旗舰系列产品的流量来源差别较大. 11 Pro的Organic + Direct在各个商城 开售后一个月 占比40%+ (除了MX商城较低32%), 11 Air各个商城基本处于20-30%.
Traffic structures vary across different stores. Based on channel analysis, traffic changes can be broadly categorized into three types:

- INTL Store: Traffic decline is almost entirely driven by a drop in paid ads, with ad contribution decreasing from 58% to 41%. Similar patterns are observed in the UK and CA stores.

- MX Store: Traffic decline comes from all channels, with the largest drop in ads, followed by Organic and YouTube.

- EU Store: Traffic growth is mainly driven by increases in ads and influencer (KOL) activity.

- SA Store: Traffic growth is primarily driven by KOLs.

- BR Store: Traffic growth is KOL-led, with both KOL and Organic traffic increasing simultaneously, alongside strong growth in ad traffic.
Overall, traffic structures across stores remain healthy, with paid ads contributing around 40–50%. Notably, the Brazil store has the lowest ad share at 14%, where traffic growth is strongly driven by KOLs.
There is also a clear difference in traffic sources between the Air sub-flagship series and the Pro flagship series. For the 11 Pro, Organic + Direct traffic accounts for over 40% across most stores in the first month after launch (except MX at 32%). In contrast, the 11 Air generally sees Organic + Direct contribution in the range of 20–30% across stores
Details

# Website & Campaign Performance Summary...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:29, y:64]**
Website & Campaign Performance Summary

- Traffic and Subscription: 11 Air Teasing & Launch Performance

  - Overall: Site traffic and subscriptions significantly exceeded 10 Air, showing stronger pre-launch strategy and product appeal.

  - Core Markets (NA & EU): Highest volumes with a balanced mix of new and returning users. Growth relies on existing users; new user share can be further improved.

  - Growth Markets (MX & BR): High new user proportion indicates strong acquisition potential. LATAM remains in a user acquisition phase, with brand awareness still developing.

  - Bottleneck Markets (UK & CA): UK faces weak new user growth; CA has limited traffic scale. Both markets need stronger user acquisition and expanded reach.

- 11 Air EBO total sales reached 2,310 units, marking a +132% increase vs. 10 Air, with all regions showing clear growth. The overall EBO target achievement reached 94%.

- 11 Air EA total sales reached 1,233 units, with 1,195 units redeemed via discount codes, representing a +191% increase vs. 10 Air. 

- For users who did not redeem early bird offers (high-intent users), consider re-engaging them through follow-up lifecycle emails or promotional campaigns, using returning discounts or installment payment options to drive conversion.

- Brazil introduced the EBO mechanism during the Launch phase for the first time, delivering strong results—especially for markets with later go-live and longer pre-launch periods.（Higher pre-launch subscription rate in Brazil indicates stronger user interest.）

- The loyal user mechanism was updated to support purchases during Early Access, delivering strong results.

# Campaign (Teasing ~ Launch)    2026.01.20 - 2026.0...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
订阅
新用户44617，提升5.5%，订阅率6.3%，降低11.3%
由于活动周期少了6天，所以订阅用户的增长并不多，只有5.5%。
订阅留存率下降11.3%，主要是因为RM7SPro活动期间需要强制用户订阅才能参与游戏，但是对用户体验并不友好，所以在8系列的活动中都没有进行强制订阅。且游戏奖励也有所减少，用户的感兴趣程度降低。


活动页和游戏
RM8Pro发布期间的游戏机制被证实是成功的，所以我们在RM8SPro上沿用了相同的机制，并且数据创下了新高。
从Teasing - Launch，参与游戏的用户有18001人，其中34%（6103人）将游戏分享到了社媒，让更多的社媒用户看到了我们的活动，分享率比RM8Pro高了40%。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:42, y:57]**
Campaign (Teasing ~ Launch)    2026.01.20 - 2026.02.05 vs 2024.11.13 - 2024.12.12

Source: GA4 + Convbox+Klaviyo

**[position: x:46, y:127]**
| 11 Air | All Store Traffic | All sign-ups (Total) | New Subs (Total) | Subscription Rate<br>(All subs/Traffic) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Teasing | 914801 | 24,551 | 16,033 | 2.8% |
| vs 10 Air | 74% | 17% | 22% | -11% |
| Launch | 867,636 | 34,129 | 26,469 | 3.9% |
| vs 10 Air | 49% | 49% | 95% | - |

**[position: x:549, y:127]**
总体：
预热和launch期间的全站流量和订阅用户明显高于 10 Air，说明本次前期预热策略及产品吸引力均优于上一代，对用户关注度的拉动更强。

订阅核心区域：北美（NA）& 欧洲（EU）
NA 与 EU 订阅总量最高，且新老用户占比相对均衡。
洞察：成熟市场中老用户基数较大，当前增长更多依赖存量用户，同时仍具备一定拉新能力，但新增用户占比仍有提升空间。

“新流量”增长区域：墨西哥（MX）& 巴西（BR）
MX 与 BR 的新用户占比较高，拉新特征明显。
巴西由于预热周期较长，但预热期间对外推广有限，新增用户增长相对受限。
洞察：11 Air 在拉美市场整体仍处于拉新阶段，品牌认知正在逐步建立。随着投放和曝光加强，未来增长潜力较大

转化与规模瓶颈区域：英国（UK）& 加拿大（CA

- UK：新用户增长乏力，整体订阅规模偏低

- CA：订阅率较高，但总量有限
洞察：

- UK 当前主要问题在于拉新不足，需加强流量获取

- CA 则受限于流量规模，需要扩大入口与曝光

订阅

订阅留存率下降11.3%，主要是因为RM7SPro活动期间需要强制用户订阅才能参与游戏，但是对用户体验并不友好，所以在8系列的活动中都没有进行强制订阅。且游戏奖励也有所减少，用户的感兴趣程度降低。


活动页和游戏

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:42, y:60]**
Campaign (Teasing ~ Launch)    2026.01.20 - 2026.02.05 vs 2024.11.13 - 2024.12.12

Source: GA4 + Convbox+Klaviyo

**[position: x:558, y:103]**
Overall
During the preheat and launch phases, overall site traffic and total subscribers significantly exceeded those of the 10 Air. This indicates that both the pre-launch strategy and product appeal outperformed the previous generation, driving stronger user interest and engagement.

Core Subscription Markets: North America (NA) & Europe (EU)
NA and EU recorded the highest subscription volumes, with a relatively balanced mix of new and returning users.
Insight:
In mature markets, a large base of existing users continues to drive growth. While there is still some ability to acquire new users, the share of new users has room for further improvement.

New Traffic Growth Markets: Mexico (MX) & Brazil (BR)
MX and BR show a higher proportion of new users, indicating strong acquisition momentum.
In Brazil, despite a longer preheat period, limited external promotion during this phase constrained new user growth to some extent.
Insight:
The LATAM region remains in a user acquisition phase for the 11 Air, with brand awareness still being established. With increased marketing investment and exposure, there is strong future growth potential.

Bottleneck Markets (Conversion & Scale): UK & Canada (CA)

- UK: Weak new user growth and relatively low overall subscription volume

- CA: Higher subscription rate but limited total volume
Insight:

- UK’s key challenge lies in insufficient user acquisition, requiring stronger traffic generation

- CA is constrained by traffic scale, and needs broader reach and exposure channels

**[position: x:39, y:316]**
11 Air EBO阶段整体销量为2310，较 10 Air 实现 +132% 显著增长，各站点相较 10 Air 阶段均有明显提升。EBO目标完成率达到 94%。
北美 & 墨西哥的激增：

- 11 Air 在北美和墨西哥的早鸟券售出比例大幅提升。说明这些地区用户对 11 Air 的性能迭代展示出了极高的兴趣。

- 欧洲依旧是销售最多的区域：虽然 11 Air 在美国的份额增长最快，但从早鸟券的绝对数量来看，欧洲依然是销售量最大的地区。说明欧洲依然有相当一部分是价格敏感性或者对次旗舰机型感兴趣的用户。
低目标达成区域：UK & 中东

- 英国: UK 早鸟券销售相较 10 Air 增长幅度有限。结合订阅数据来看，新用户获取不足，流量规模偏小 > 需加强投放与用户获取能力。

- GL 相较 10 Air 增长幅度较小：主要由于本次流量更加精准，非可售地区或被其他站点（如 US）承接的早鸟销售占比下降，导致整体规模增长相对较少。

- 中东地区目标达成率偏低： 可能与当地用户对旗舰机型偏好更高有关，当前产品吸引力相对有限。

# Campaign (EBO Sales Performance)...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
订阅
新用户44617，提升5.5%，订阅率6.3%，降低11.3%
由于活动周期少了6天，所以订阅用户的增长并不多，只有5.5%。
订阅留存率下降11.3%，主要是因为RM7SPro活动期间需要强制用户订阅才能参与游戏，但是对用户体验并不友好，所以在8系列的活动中都没有进行强制订阅。且游戏奖励也有所减少，用户的感兴趣程度降低。


活动页和游戏
RM8Pro发布期间的游戏机制被证实是成功的，所以我们在RM8SPro上沿用了相同的机制，并且数据创下了新高。
从Teasing - Launch，参与游戏的用户有18001人，其中34%（6103人）将游戏分享到了社媒，让更多的社媒用户看到了我们的活动，分享率比RM8Pro高了40%。

Overall Performance
11 Air EBO total sales reached 2,310 units, marking a +132% increase vs. 10 Air, with all regions showing clear growth. The overall EBO target achievement reached 94%.
Surging Markets: North America & Mexico
The share of early bird sales in NA and MX increased significantly, indicating strong user interest in the 11 Air’s performance upgrades.
Largest Volume Market: Europe
While the US saw the fastest growth, Europe still contributed the highest absolute EBO volume, remaining the largest demand pool. This suggests a relatively higher share of price-sensitive users in the region.
Underperforming Markets: UK & Middle East
UK: Limited growth vs. 10 Air. Subscription data indicates weak new user acquisition and low traffic scale → further investment in traffic and user acquisition is needed.
GL: Slower growth vs. 10 Air, mainly due to more targeted traffic. A reduced share of users from non-sellable regions or those redirected to other sites (e.g., US) limited overall volume growth.
Middle East: Lower target achievement, possibly due to a stronger preference for flagship models, resulting in relatively weaker product appeal.

11 Air EA total sales reached 1,233 units, with 1,195 units redeemed via discount codes, representing a +191% increase vs. 10 Air. All regions showed clear improvements compared to the 10 Air phase. At the 14-hour mark, EA completion reached 141%, exceeding the target by 110% overall. The overall Used Rate was 52%, indicating that most EA codes were effectively utilized (excluding trace). This marks a +35% increase vs. the 10 Air EBO redemption rate (38.23%). For users who did not redeem early bird offers (high-intent users), consider re-engaging them through follow-up lifecycle emails or promotional campaigns, using returning discounts or installment payment options to drive conversion.

订阅

活动页和游戏
RM8Pro发布期间的游戏机制被证实是成功的，所以我们在RM8SPro上沿用了相同的机制，并且数据创下了新高。
从Teasing - Launch，参与游戏的用户有18001人，其中34%（6103人）将游戏分享到了社媒，让更多的社媒用户看到了我们的活动，分享率比RM8Pro高了40%。

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:42, y:51]**
Campaign (EBO) Brazil Traffic & CRV Comparison

**[position: x:54, y:110]**
| BR EBO Period | Traffic | All Store | Launch page Pageviews | Launch page CRV | EBO Sales |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 11 Air<br>(Launch&EBO) | 47,748 | 0.42% | 27,585<br>*Contains 11-air | 0.74% | 189 |
| 11 Pro | 31,550 | 0.88% | 36,410<br>*Contains 11-pro | 0.67% | 326 |
| 10 Air | 35,642 | 0.26% | 35,539<br>*Contains 10-air | 0.29% | 79 |

**[position: x:49, y:294]**
本次巴西市场首次在 Launch 阶段引入早鸟券（EBO）机制，整体表现良好，尤其适用于上线时间较GL晚、预热周期较长的市场。（GL站中预热到Launch期间巴西流量18198，比10 Air期间增长300%（4,086）；巴西站预热期间订阅率较高，说明用户兴趣度较高）
从流量表现来看，巴西在本次 Launch & EBO 阶段的整体流量均高于 11 Pro 和 10 Air，同时用户平均停留时长提升至 112 秒，用户参与度更高。
从转化路径来看，11 Air早鸟在巴西的总体转化表现优于 11 Pro 及其 EBO 阶段（0.74%）。其中，早鸟券购买页的转化率最高（1.52%），说明用户更倾向于先进入 PDP，再通过页面引导购买早鸟券。相比之下，11 Pro 和 10 Air 的最高转化页面集中在 Launch 页面，表明高意愿用户更倾向在 EBO 开始时，通过召回或其他渠道直接进入 Launch 页面完成转化。
此外，本次巴西在 EBO 阶段首次叠加赠品策略，对销售增长也起到了明显促进作用。11 Air 在巴西的早鸟券销量较 10 Air 提升 130%。

# Integrated Marketing Communications (IMC) Summary...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
REDMAGIC 11 Air 在品牌认知、媒体表现和整体转化效率表现相比 10 Air 有明显提升。先看投放表现： Google 广告收入达到 167.6 万美元，增长约 91%；Meta 收入 82.6 万美元，增长约 54%，整体 ROI 明显提升，主要得益于 Air 系列品牌认知度的提升。
媒体端： 媒体评分从 7.9 提升到 9，30 天内发布率也从 44% 提升到 58%。同时进入了多类“最佳轻薄游戏手机”和“高性价比游戏设备”榜单，产品定位认可度更强。
社媒表现： 整体表现全面增长，reach 提升 81%，engagement 提升 78%，新增粉丝增长到 37.8 万，说明从曝光到转化的效率都有提升。用户对 11 Air 的反馈也更积极，主要来自设计语言强化以及 Turbo Fan 回归带来的性能认知增强。
联盟营销： 虽然佣金率相比 10 Air 下降约 21%，但从 EBO 到开售阶段，联盟收入和兴趣反而增长了 267%，说明整体转化效率更高。
预热阶段数据： 网站访问量增长 7.9%，新用户增长 47%，订阅增长 32%；博客点击提升 96%，曝光提升 237%，平均排名也从 11 提升到 5.7，说明前期内容拉动效果更强。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:28, y:55]**
Integrated Marketing Communications (IMC) Summary
The REDMAGIC 11 Air campaign demonstrated significant growth across both Google and Meta platforms. On Google, the campaign generated $1,675,754 in revenue from an ad spend of $52,849, marking a 91.2% increase in revenue compared to the 10 Air campaign. On Meta, the 11 Air campaign generated $826,223 in revenue from an ad spend of $11,832, representing a 53.8% increase. This growth is largely attributed to stronger brand recognition of the new Air product line, with the 10 Air serving as the initial test product.
Media ratings for the 11 Air increased from 7.9 to 9, and the publication rate within the first 30 days increased from 44% to 58%, reflecting stronger press engagement. The 11 Air has also been featured in “best lightweight gaming phone” and “top budget gaming device” roundups, signaling its success in balancing performance with portability.
The 11 Air campaign outperformed the 10 Air across all metrics on social media, with reach increasing by 81% (89.5M vs. 49.4M) and engagement up by 78% (4.4M vs. 2.47M). Audience growth surged to 378K new followers, compared to 312K for the 10 Air, showing improved conversion from reach to community. Fans responded more positively to the 11 Air, driven by its stronger REDMAGIC design identity and the return of the Turbo Fan, which reinforced its performance credibility.
In terms of affiliate marketing, the commission rate for the 11 Air decreased by 21% compared to the 10 Air, but affiliate interest and revenue increased by 267% from the EBO stage to open sales.
Pre-launch data for the REDMAGIC 11 Air also showed significant growth in core metrics, with total visits rising by 7.94% and new users and subscriptions increasing by 47.08% and 32.65%, respectively. Compared to the 10 Air, the 11 Air showed a 96% increase in blog clicks (65.2K), and impressions grew by 237% (4.01M). The average ranking for blog content improved from 11 to 5.7, reflecting stronger consumer interest and visibility for the 11 Air.

# Digital Ads: REDMAGIC 11 Air Versus 10 Air...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
整体来看，11 Air 在两个渠道的表现都明显更好，而且效率提升很明显。
Google这边，预算基本没变，只涨了2%，但营收增长了91%，ROAS从17提升到32，基本翻倍。
Meta这边更明显，预算下降了接近30%，但营收反而增长了54%。
核心原因一方面是10 Air已经帮我们做了市场教育，另一方面是这一轮我们在KOL、PR曝光，还有人群定向和素材上都做了优化，所以整体投放效率提升得很明显。

REDMAGIC 11 Air 在 Google 渠道的投放表现出色，以 $52,849 的广告投入带来了 $1,675,754 的营收。这相比 10 Air（$876,055）实现了 91.2% 的营收增长。值得注意的是，在广告投入仅增长 2.1% 的情况下，11 Air 实现了接近翻倍的收入，投放效率显著提升，使 ROAS 从 17 提升至 32。

Meta:

**[position: x:25, y:356]**
Google: 
The REDMAGIC 11 Air campaign on Google demonstrated exceptional growth, generating $1,675,754 in revenue from an ad spend of $52,849 (This represents a 91.2% increase in revenue compared to the 10 Air campaign, which earned $876,055. Notably, the 11 Air achieved nearly double the revenue of its predecessor while maintaining a nearly identical budget (only a 2.1% increase in spend). This efficiency resulted in a massive leap in Return on Ad Spend (ROAS), jumping from 17 to 32. 

Meta: 
The 11 Air campaign delivered outstanding results, generating $826,223 in revenue from an ad spend of $11,832. By comparison, the 10 Air campaign achieved $537,241 in revenue, representing a 53.8% increase for the 11 Air series. This uplift is primarily attributed to stronger recognition of the new Air product line, with the 10 Air serving as the pioneer to test the market. The 11 Air campaign operated with a 29.8% lower ad spend yet achieved a 53.8% higher revenue, underscoring the impact of both market recognition and a successful advertising strategy. This strategy combined KOL collaborations, PR media exposure, precise audience targeting, and optimized creative assets.

# Search Engine Ads: 11 Air Awareness 260120-260311...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
Awareness 目前，Google 占比40%，Social 占比68%。
Google Demand Gen 效果最好，量大成本低，是主力渠道。 Meta 和 TikTok 表现稳定，素材和互动都不错。 Microsoft 成本相对高一些但流量质量好。 Reddit 和 Snapchat 各有优势，一个成本低，一个更偏精准触达目标年轻群体。
今后预算建议 Google Demand Gen综合投放效果最好. 优先加码 Google，Reddit 和 Snapchat 流量质量好, 按预算规模灵活分配。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:136, y:4]**
Search Engine Ads: 11 Air Awareness 260120-260311

**[position: x:14, y:53]**
|  | 预算 Cost | 触达人群 Reach | 引流 Traffic |
|---|---|---|---|
| Impr. | CPM | Clicks | CPC | CTR | Landing Page Views | Bounce Rate | Avg. Session Duration |
| Google Demand Gen | $26,698 | 31,435,186 | $0.85 | 770,737 | $0.03 | 2.45% | NA | 29% | 0:01:46 |
| Microsoft Traffic Ads | $1,251 | 4,687,239 | $3.32 | 1,701 | $2.7 | 0.80% | NA | 41% | 0:01:59 |
| Meta Traffic | $6,946.66 | 5,284,193 | $1.31 | 234,208 | $0.03 | 4.43% | 138,846 | 44% | 0:01:23 |
| TikTok Traffic | $3,484.73 | 835,127 | $4.17 | 27,227 | $0.28 | 3.26% | 7,855 | 65% | 0:01:02 |
| Reddit Cost (Lifetime Data) | $1,997.98 | 348,991 | $5.73 | 12,666 | $0.16 | 3.62% | 2,149 | 77% | 0:00:40 |
| Snapchat Cost (Lifetime Data) | $4,420.00 | 383,014 | $11.54 | 5,572 | $0.79 | 1.45% | 3,673 | 56% | 0:01:19 |

**[position: x:7, y:202]**
预算结构: Awareness 广告占 Google 总广告预算的 40%，占 Social 广告预算的 68%。

**[position: x:14, y:229]**
Google（Demand Gen）分析：

- 高效率： 该活动以较低的 CPM（$0.85）实现了超过 3100 万次曝光，规模巨大。

- 用户互动： CTR 达到 2.45%，在展示类广告中表现非常强劲，带来了超过 77 万次点击。

- 成本效率： CPC 约为 $0.03，说明该活动在获取大规模流量方面具有极高的效率。
Microsoft（Traffic）分析：

- 成本较高： CPM（$3.32）和 CPC（$2.70）显著高于 Google。

- 流量质量： 尽管成本更高，但平均会话时长（1:59）略高于 Google（1:46），表明流量质量相对更好。
Meta： 内部制作的视频内容（包括生活方式、渲染图和开箱首看）表现最佳，自预热阶段以来 CTR 达到 4.43%，同时实现最低 CPC（$0.03），体现出极强的用户互动能力和成本效率。
TikTok： Spark Ads 保持稳定的高 CTR（3.26% 以上），CPM 为 $4.17，与 NEU 区域基准一致。通过轮播来自社媒内容的 Spark Ads，有效缓解广告疲劳，并精准触达核心人群（如科技爱好者、消费者、游戏玩家及竞品用户）。
Reddit： Reddit 实现了超过 3.62% 的 CTR，表现亮眼。主要由 Mora 风格和 meme 创意驱动，这类更偏沟通型的内容强化了 Reddit 在触达游戏垂直社区用户方面的价值。
Snapchat： Snapchat 表现良好，CTR 达到 1.45%。虽然 CPM 相对较高，但在精准触达 Gen Z 用户方面具有优势，同时 CPC 仍具竞争力（$0.79）。通过使用图片广告并扩大受众范围，可以在保持 CTR 的同时降低 CPM。

预算建议：Google Demand Gen综合投放效果最好.  在 Reddit 与 Snapchat 的预算分配上，两者均具备投放价值，主要取决于预算规模。Snapchat 在跳出率和平均停留时长方面优于 Reddit，但 CPM 更高。整体来看，建议在两个平台上持续进行 Awareness 投放，以覆盖更广泛的受众群体。

# Search Engine Ads: 11 Air Awareness 260120-260311...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:136, y:4]**
Search Engine Ads: 11 Air Awareness 260120-260311

**[position: x:14, y:53]**
|  | Cost | Reach | Traffic |
|---|---|---|---|
| Impr. | CPM | Clicks | CPC | CTR | Landing Page Views | Bounce Rate | Avg. Session Duration |
| Google Demand Gen | $26,698 | 31,435,186 | $0.85 | 770,737 | $0.03 | 2.45% | NA | 29% | 0:01:46 |
| Microsoft Traffic Ads | $1,251 | 4,687,239 | $3.32 | 1,701 | $2.7 | 0.80% | NA | 41% | 0:01:59 |
| Meta Traffic | $6,946.66 | 5,284,193 | $1.31 | 234,208 | $0.03 | 4.43% | 138,846 | 44% | 0:01:23 |
| TikTok Traffic | $3,484.73 | 835,127 | $4.17 | 27,227 | $0.28 | 3.26% | 7,855 | 65% | 0:01:02 |
| Reddit Cost (Lifetime Data) | $1,997.98 | 348,991 | $5.73 | 12,666 | $0.16 | 3.62% | 2,149 | 77% | 0:00:40 |
| Snapchat Cost (Lifetime Data) | $4,420.00 | 383,014 | $11.54 | 5,572 | $0.79 | 1.45% | 3,673 | 56% | 0:01:19 |

**[position: x:7, y:202]**
Awareness Ads accounts for 40% total Google ad budget and 68% total Social Ads budget

**[position: x:14, y:226]**
Google (Demand Gen) Analysis

- High Efficiency: This campaign is operating at a massive scale with over 31 million impressions at a low CPM ($0.85).

- Engagement: The CTR (2.45%) is very strong for a display-style campaign, resulting in over 770,000 clicks.

- Cost-Effectiveness: The CPC is essentially $0.03, suggesting this campaign is highly optimized for driving high volumes.
Microsoft (Traffic) Analysis

- Higher Costs: The CPM ($3.32) and CPC ($2.70) are significantly higher than Google's.

- Traffic Quality: While it is more expensive, the Avg. Session Duration (1:59) is slightly higher than Google's (1:46). 

Meta: Internal production videos, including lifestyle, render, and first-look formats, delivered the strongest performance, achieving a CTR of 4.43% since the start of teasing and the lowest CPC at $0.03. These results highlight exceptional audience engagement and cost efficiency.  

TikTok: Spark Ads maintained a consistently high CTR above 3.26% with a stable CPM of $4.17, aligned with NEU regional benchmarks. To mitigate ad fatigue and maximize impact, a rotation of Spark Ads sourced from social media was launched, effectively engaging key audience segments such as geeks, shoppers, gamers, and competitor-focused audiences.  

Reddit: Reddit achieved a CTR above 3.62%, marking a significant success milestone. Performance was driven by Mora and meme-style creatives, which function as more communication-oriented ads, reinforcing Reddit’s value as a platform for reaching niche audiences within the gaming community.  

Snapchat: Snapchat demonstrated promising results with a CTR of 1.45%. While CPM was higher compared to other platforms, the channel ensured precise targeting of Gen Z audiences, supported by a competitive CPC of $0.79. We identified a way to reduce CPM while maintaining CTR by using image ads and expanding audience targeting.  

Budget Considerations: Overall Google Demend Gen is the most effective. For investment allocation between Reddit and Snapchat, we concluded that both platforms are viable, with budget being the primary concern. Snapchat outperformed Reddit in bounce rate and average session duration, though CPM remains higher. Overall, maintaining awareness campaigns on both Reddit and Snapchat is recommended to reach diverse audience segments effectively.

# Search Engine Ads: 11 Air Conversion 260120-260311...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
转化预算这块，Google 占 60%，社交占 32%。
Google 依然是核心，ROAS 达到 32，转化量最大，是主要收入来源。 Microsoft 表现偏弱，流量质量和转化都比较低。
Meta 表现不错，尤其 NEU 市场 ROAS 很高，是重点市场；常规 Meta 在11 Air降预算的同时效率反而提升。
TikTok 这次转化也跑出来了，ROAS 到 70，表现超预期。
结论: 建议继续以 Google 为核心，Meta 和 TikTok 做重点补充，Microsoft 可适当收缩。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:137, y:0]**
Search Engine Ads: 11 Air Conversion 260120-260311

**[position: x:17, y:58]**
| Type | ROAS | Sales | Traffic |
|---|---|---|---|
| Revenue | Spent | Purchases | Adds to cart | CPM | Impression | CPC | CTR | Bounce Rate | Avg. Session <br>Duration |
| Google PMAX Search | 32 | $1,628,825 | $26,150 | 2743 | 18525 | 19.59 | 1,338,832 | $0.19 | 10% | 21% | 0:03:24 |
| Microsoft NEU | 5 | $4,336 | $1,518 | 12 | 27 | $3.65 | 472,509 | $0.42 | 0.76% | 49% | 0:01:45 |
| Meta NEU | 153 | $266,885.97 | $1,740 | 402 | 3,507 | $8.21 | 211,783 | $0.48 | 4.46% | 68.50% | 0:00:56 |
| Meta Non-NEU | 151 | $476,313.84 | $3,146 | 876 | 7,732 | $3.27 | 960,799 | $0.08 | 3.95% | 43.85% | 0:01:23 |
| TikTok | 70 | $203,002.10 | $2,900 | 338 |  | $2.37 | 1,222,616 | $0.20 | 2.48% | 64.84% | 0:01:02 |

**[position: x:17, y:197]**
转化广告预算结构：Google 广告占总预算的 60%，社交广告占 32%

**[position: x:19, y:219]**
Google 分析：

- 高 ROAS： ROAS 达到 32 倍，高于上一代产品线，回报极高。

- 用户质量： CTR 达到 10%，平均会话时长为 3:24，跳出率稳定。

- 转化能力： 实现 2,743 笔购买和 18,525 次加购，是业务收入的核心驱动力。

Microsoft 分析：

- 中等 ROAS： ROAS 为 5.06，虽具备盈利能力，但表现较弱。

- 流量质量较低： CTR 仅 0.76%，跳出率达 49%。

- 用户兴趣较低： 平均停留时长仅 1:45，不到 Google 的一半，导致转化量较低（仅 12 单）。

NEU Meta 账户： 作为独立转化广告账户，在 NEU 市场表现突出，ROAS 达到 153，CTR 为 4.46%。这反映出该区域强劲的购买力，仍是核心市场。尽管因聚焦转化人群导致 CPM 较高，但跳出率和平均停留时长仍处于基准水平。

常规 Meta： 相比 10 Air，Meta 在 11 Air 上实现了更强的营收表现，同时预算减少了 29.8%。在降低投入的同时 ROAS 反而提升，说明 Air 系列在 10 Air 上市后已逐步建立市场认知。该活动聚焦核心人群（科技爱好者、消费者、游戏玩家及竞品用户），CTR 达到 3.95%，对于转化类广告而言表现优异，体现出较强的用户参与度和购买意图。

TikTok： Smart Performance（智能投放）广告通过算法驱动的自动定向，实现了 2.48% 的 CTR 和 70 的 ROAS。相比以曝光为主的 Spark Ads，其 CPM 更低。在维持曝光量的同时，实现了具有竞争力的转化表现，并触达与 TikTok 品宣广告不同的人群，进一步验证了 TikTok 在转化导向上的价值。

**[position: x:137, y:0]**

**[position: x:17, y:58]**
| Type | ROAS | Sales | Traffic |
|---|---|---|---|
| Revenue | Spent | Purchases | Adds to cart | CPM | Impression | CPC | CTR | Bounce Rate | Avg. Session <br>Duration |
| Google PMAX Search | 32 | $1,628,825 | $26,150 | 2743 | 18525 | 19.59 | 1,338,832 | $0.19 | 10% | 21% | 0:03:24 |
| Microsoft NEU | 5 | $4,336 | $1,518 | 12 | 27 | $3.65 | 472,509 | $0.42 | 0.76% | 49% | 0:01:45 |
| Meta NEU | 153 | $266,885.97 | $1,740 | 402 | 3,507 | $8.21 | 211,783 | $0.48 | 4.46% | 68.50% | 0:00:56 |
| Meta Non-NEU | 151 | $476,313.84 | $3,146 | 876 | 7,732 | $3.27 | 960,799 | $0.08 | 3.95% | 43.85% | 0:01:23 |

**[position: x:17, y:197]**
60% total Google ad budget and 32% total social ads budget

**[position: x:19, y:219]**
Google Analysis

- High ROAS and higher than the previous product line. The campaign achieved a ROAS of 32x. High return. 

- 10% CTR and an Avg. Session Duration of 3:24 & bounce rate is stable. 

- Conversion Volume: With 2,743 purchases and 18,525 adds to cart, this campaign is the primary driver of the business's bottom line.

Microsoft Analysis

- Modest ROAS 5.06x. Profitable but lower traffic quality: The 0.76% CTR and 49% bounce rate. Shorter Interest: Users spend less than half the time on the site (1:45) compared to Google users, resulting in much lower purchase volume (only 12 total purchases).

NEU Meta Account: The NEU Meta account, operating as a standalone conversion campaign, achieved an impressive ROAS of 153 and a CTR of 4.46% in the NEU market. This indicates strong purchasing power in the region, which remains our primary market. Although CPM is higher due to the focus on conversion audiences, bounce rate and average session duration remain within benchmark levels.

Regular Meta: Meta is demonstrating stronger revenue performance compared to the 10 Air campaigns, particularly with a 29.8% budget reduction on 11 Air. ROAS has increased as spending decreased, and the Air series continues to gain recognition in the market following the launch of 10 Air. The campaign focuses on core audience segments, geeks, shoppers, gamers, and competitor audiences. A CTR of 3.95% is high for conversion ads, reflecting strong engagement and clear purchase intent.

TikTok: Smart Performance campaign, leveraging algorithm-driven automated targeting, delivered a CTR of 2.48% and a solid ROAS of 70. CPM was lower compared to awareness-focused Spark Ads. The campaign maintained impressions while driving competitive purchase rates, targeting audiences distinct from TikTok’s awareness ads. This reinforces TikTok’s value as a conversion-focused channel.

# Digital Ads: Audience Segments - Search Engine Ads...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
搜索引擎广告在预售认知阶段的受众表现分析在预售的认知阶段，我们主要拉高流量，为正式开售建立加购基础。
Google表现： 核心人群最稳定，贡献最多收入和加购； 相似人群成本低、效率高，是触达新用户的补充； 科技极客互动强，CTR 高，但转化较低。
Microsoft表现： 再营销用户最有效，成本低反应快； Geek人群表现稳健，获取高质量用户； Gamer群体扩量能力强，CPM低，适合大规模曝光。
结论： Google主力拉核心和相似人群，Microsoft主要做再营销和扩量补充

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:137, y:5]**
Digital Ads: Audience Segments - Search Engine Ads

**[position: x:557, y:52]**
Awareness 阶段表现分析 在预售的认知阶段，我们加大预算投入，以获取高规模流量，并在正式开售前建立稳定的“加购（ATC）”基础。

关键洞察：

- 核心人群主导： 核心人群（由再营销人群和游戏玩家构成）表现最佳，实现最高收入（$19,997）。更重要的是，该人群贡献了 1,621 次加购，是最稳定的中层转化
（mid-funnel）驱动力。

- 相似人群效率高： Lookalike 人群以 2.3 的 ROAS 紧随其后。虽然规模不及核心人群，但其 CPM 仅为 $0.70，是成本效率最高的人群，是扩大触达的重要补充。

- 科技极客高互动： Tech Geek 人群虽然 ROAS 最低
（0.9），但 CTR 最高（2.83%），说明创意内容在吸引科技爱好者方面具有较强吸引力。

**[position: x:321, y:92]**
612 × 408

**[position: y:153]**
| Lookalike | Core Audience | Tech |
|---|---|---|

**[position: x:32, y:213]**
| Google Ads | Spent | Traffic (Clicks) | ROAS | Revenue | ATC | CPM | CPC | CTR |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 相似人群 Lookalike | $4802 | 188,478 | 2.3 | $11,239 | 638 | $0.70 | $0.78 | 2.73% |
| 核心用户 Core Audience | $7573 | 230,402 | 2.6 | $19,997 | 1621 | $0.74 | $0.03 | 2.24% |
| 极客 Tech Geek | $9247 | 293,737 | 0.9 | $8799 | 602 | $0.89 | $0.03 | 2.83% |

**[position: x:557, y:292]**
Microsoft：


- 再营销表现最佳： Remarketing 广告组表现突出，CTR 达到 1.18%，CPC 最低为 $0.29，说明曾与品牌互动过的用户在该平台上响应度最高，带来最具成本效率的流量。

- Geek 人群互动稳定： Geek 人群 CTR 为 0.84%，表现稳健。虽然 CPM 处于中等水平（$3.22），但依然是获取高质量科技用户的重要来源。

- Gamer 人群扩量能力强： Gamer 广告组拥有最具竞争力的 CPM（$3.18）。尽管 CTR 最低（0.66%），但在低成本获取大规模曝光方面表现出色，是品牌曝光的重要补充渠道。

**[position: x:32, y:309]**
| Microsoft Ad<br>Ad group | CPM | CPC | CTR |
|---|---|---|---|
| 极客 Geek | $3.22 | 0.39 | 0.84% |
| 游戏玩家 Gamer | $3.18 | 0.48 | 0.66% |
| 重定向老用户  Remarketing | $3.39 | 0.29 | 1.18% |

## 🖼️ Elements Position

**[position: x:557, y:52]**
Awareness Phase Performance Analysis
During this pre-sale awareness stage, we aggressively allocated budget to drive high-volume traffic and build "Add to Cart" (ATC) stability before the official sales launch.

Key Insights:
Core Audience Dominance: The Core Audience (comprising Remarketing and Gamers) is our top performer. It achieved the highest Revenue ($19,997). Most importantly, it delivered a massive 1,621 ATCs, proving it is the most stable engine for mid-funnel intent.

Lookalike Efficiency: The Lookalike audience followed closely with a 2.3 ROAS. While its volume is lower than the Core Audience, its $0.70 CPM is the most cost-effective in the group, making it a vital secondary driver for reach.

Tech Geek Engagement: While the Tech Geek segment had the lowest ROAS (0.9), it achieved the highest CTR (2.83%). This indicates that our creative messaging is effective at attracting the attention of tech enthusiasts.

**[position: x:321, y:92]**

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Lookalike | $4802 | 188,478 | 2.3 | $11,239 | 638 | $0.70 | $0.78 | 2.73% |
| Core Audience | $7573 | 230,402 | 2.6 | $19,997 | 1621 | $0.74 | $0.03 | 2.24% |
| Tech Geek | $9247 | 293,737 | 0.9 | $8799 | 602 | $0.89 | $0.03 | 2.83% |

**[position: x:557, y:292]**
Microsoft
The Remarketing ad group is the clear standout, achieving the highest CTR (1.18%) and the lowest CPC ($0.29). This confirms that users who have previously engaged with the brand are highly responsive on this platform, providing the most cost-efficient traffic.

Geek Audience Engagement: The Geek audience showed solid engagement with a 0.84% CTR. While its CPM is mid-range at $3.22, it remains a valuable segment for driving qualified traffic from tech-centric users.

Gamer Reach: The Gamer ad group provided the most competitive CPM ($3.18). Although it had the lowest CTR (0.66%), it served as an effective tool for broad brand exposure at the lowest cost per thousand impressions.

# Digital Ads: Audience Segments - Social Ads...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
社交广告受众表现分析, 四个平台表现都不错：

- Meta：核心人群覆盖均衡，CTR 高、CPC 低，转化稳定。

- TikTok：Spark Ads 定向精准，CTR 高、CPC 低，CPM 稳定。

- Reddit：CTR 超 3%，meme 和 Mora 风格创意在游戏社区吸引力强。

- Snapchat：调整策略后，CTR 保持基准以上，图片广告成本效率高。
社交广告渠道各有优势，我们会继续结合人群和创意进行精准投放。

---

TikTok： 在 TikTok 上，Spark Ads 聚焦于特定人群定向。结果显示投放效果显著，CTR 较高、CPC 极低，同时 CPM 保持稳定，与平台基准一致。
Reddit： Reddit 的 CTR 超过 3%，在认知与互动层面取得重要突破。以 meme 和 Mora 风格为主的创意在游戏社区中表现尤为出色，能够有效吸引用户注意力。
Snapchat： 在 Snapchat 上，策略进行了调整，以在维持标准 CTR 的同时降低 CPM。图片广告表现出较高的成本效率，并验证了在广泛人群定向下，CTR 仍可超过基准水平。

# Google Video Ads...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
Google 视频广告方面：

- Render：拉量核心，CTR 最高，收入和购买都不错，视觉吸引力强。

- First Look：效率最高，ROAS 和 CPV 最优，小预算就能带来加购，适合放量或 Awareness。

- Lifestyle：表现较弱，CPC 高。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:129, y:4]**
Google Video Ads

**[position: x:39, y:187]**
|  | Cost | ROAS | Revenue | Purchase | ATC | CTR | CPC | CPM | CPV |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| First Look 自拍 | $141 | 3.43 | $753 | 1 | 34 | 1.60% | $0.04 | 0.70 | $0.011 |
| Render 产品渲染视频 | $7435 | 1.27 | $9443 | 19 | 442 | 3.91% | $0.04 | 1.53 | $0.025 |
| Lifestyle 模特场景视频 | $961 | 0.90 | $874 | 1 | 26 | 2.57% | $0.49 | 0.50 | $0.257 |

**[position: x:39, y:256]**
Render（高量驱动）

- 表现： 是广告的核心驱动，带来了 $9,443 的收入和 19 笔购买。

- 互动： CTR 达到最高的 3.91%，说明 “Render” 视觉在吸引注意力和引导点击方面非常有效。
First Look（效率领先）

- 盈利能力： 拥有最高的 ROAS（3.43 倍）和最低的 CPV（$0.011）。

- 洞察： 投放成本极低且转化效率高（仅用 $141 投入就带来 34 次加购），非常适合用于放量或作为上层漏斗（Awareness）的核心素材。
Lifestyle（表现较弱）

- 问题： CPC（$0.49）是另外两种创意的 10 倍以上，说明该类“生活方式”内容未能有效打动当前受众，或未清晰传达产品核心卖点。 

Render (The High-Volume Driver)

- Performance: This is the primary driver of the account, generating $9,443 in revenue and 19 purchases.

- Engagement: It boasts the highest CTR (3.91%), showing that the "Render" visual is highly effective at stopping the scroll and driving traffic.
First Look (The Efficiency Leader)

- Profitability: This creative has the highest ROAS (3.43x) and the lowest CPV ($0.011).

- Insight: It is incredibly cheaper to run and very efficient at driving initial interest (34 ATCs on just $141 spend), making it an ideal candidate for scaling or top-of-funnel awareness.
 Lifestyle (Underperformer)

- Friction: The CPC ($0.49) is more than 10x higher than the other two creatives. This suggests that the "Lifestyle" vibe isn't resonating with the current audience or isn't clearly communicating the product's value proposition.

# Digital Ads: Ad Creatives - Meta...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
Meta 广告创意策略分阶段进行：

- 预热：关键视觉和短视频，激发好奇心和期待感。

- 发布：开箱视频、渲染图、生活方式内容，结合 KOL 和媒体背书，提升认知。

- 中层转化：产品卖点和促销结合 KOL 内容，强化购买动机。

- 底层转化：再营销人群，加大投放复用高表现素材，最大化转化和收入。
结论：创意随投放阶段不断精细化，从兴趣引导到购买转化，形成完整漏斗闭环。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:137, y:5]**
Digital Ads: Ad Creatives - Meta

**[position: y:51]**
| Upper Funnel | Middle Funnel | Low Funnel |
|---|---|---|

**[position: y:80]**
| First Look/ Render/ Lifestyle / KOL | KV/ KSP/ Model / KOL | Media/ Promotion |
|---|---|---|

**[position: x:29, y:284]**
| Creative | CTR | ROAS | Spent | Revenue |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Upper Funnel | 4.43% | 12 | $6,946 | $83,023 |
| Middle Funnel | 3.70% | 137 | $2,309 | $318,220 |
| Low Funnel | 4.68% | 189 | $835 | $158,093 |

**[position: x:400, y:284]**
在预热阶段，通过发布关键视觉素材和短视频预告来激发用户的好奇心和期待感。这一策略旨在在保持神秘感的同时，引导早期兴趣。
在 Launch 发布阶段，引入了内部制作的素材，包括开箱首看视频、产品渲染图和生活方式内容。同时延续以往策略，提前投放 KOL 内容和媒体背书，提升用户认知与教育，从而加速购买意愿的形成。
在中层转化阶段，通过产品导向的视觉内容（如设计与帧率亮点）结合 KOL 叙事和合作内容，强化产品卖点。同时突出主机联动能力等核心功能，并配合促销信息，进一步增强购买动机并推动转化。
在底层转化阶段，策略聚焦于再营销人群。相较于发布阶段加大投放力度，通过复用高表现素材建立用户信任，最大化转化效果与收入表现。

**[position: x:401, y:266]**
During the teaser phase, key visuals and short teaser videos were released to build curiosity and anticipation. This approach was designed to spark early interest while maintaining an element of surprise.

At launch, internally produced assets, including first-look videos, product renders, and lifestyle content, were introduced. As in previous campaigns, KOL content and media endorsements were deployed early to provide educational value and accelerate purchase intent.

Mid-funnel efforts combined product-focused imagery, such as design and frame rate highlights, with KOL-led storytelling and partnerships. Core features, including console capability, were emphasized alongside promotional offers, reinforcing purchase motivation and driving revenue.

Lower-funnel strategies concentrated on retargeting audiences. Investment was increased from the launch stage, leveraging high-performing media to build trust and maximize conversion and revenue impact.

# Meta Video Ads...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
Meta 视频广告方面：

- CPC 低，效率高，Render 视频 CTR 最高 7.18%。

- 与Google Ads表现不同, Lifestyle 视频在social ads表现最优，融合 Render 风格，带来 64 笔购买和 1,808 次加购，因此获得更高预算。

- 所有视频形式都用于转化和引流广告，根据 CTR 和收入表现持续优化。

- 目前四种视频持续投放，覆盖不同用户群体，确保整体效果最优。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:129, y:8]**
Meta Video Ads

**[position: x:14, y:61]**
|  | Cost | ROAS | Purchase | ATC | CTR | CPC |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| First Look | $893 | 8 | 11 | 256 | 4.73% | $0.02 |
| Render | $865 | 15 | 25 | 365 | 7.18% | $0.03 |
| Lifestyle | $1,692 | 23 | 64 | 1,080 | 5.60% | $0.06 |
| Hands-On | $496 | 12 | 8 | 341 | 6.53% | $0.03 |

**[position: x:9, y:159]**
各类视频形式的 CPC 均保持在较低水平，在触达用户和投放效率方面表现优异。其中，Lifestyle 视频的 CPC 略高，为 $0.06，而 Render 视频则实现了最高的 CTR（7.18%）。整体 ROAS 较低，主要是因为预算更多分配在以引流为主的广告上，这部分占社交广告总预算的约 68%。
所有视频形式均同时应用于转化和引流广告中，并根据后期的 CTR 和收入表现进行优化调整。
Lifestyle 视频表现最为突出，在整体互动和转化指标中排名领先，实现了 64 笔购买和 1,808 次加购。其优异表现主要得益于融入了 Render 风格的创意元素。过往数据也验证了以 Render 为核心的内容在转化方面的重要性，这也是 Lifestyle 视频获得更高预算倾斜的原因，因为其持续带来最佳效果。
目前四种视频形式均保持投放，以覆盖不同用户人群，从而实现整体投放效果的最优表现。
CPC remained consistently low across all video formats, maximizing reach and efficiency with audiences. Lifestyle videos showed a slightly higher CPC at $0.06, while render videos achieved the highest CTR at 7.18%. The lower ROAS observed was primarily due to heavier allocation within traffic campaigns, which accounted for approximately 68% of the total social ads budget.

All video formats were featured in both conversion and traffic campaigns, with adjustments made later based on CTR and revenue outcomes.

Lifestyle videos delivered outstanding results, ranking highest in overall engagement and conversion metrics, with 64 purchases and 1,808 add-to-cart actions. Their strong performance can be attributed to render-focused creative elements. Past data reinforces the importance of render-driven content for conversions, which explains the higher budget allocation to lifestyle videos, as they consistently deliver the strongest outcomes.

All four video formats remain active, ensuring coverage across diverse audience segments and driving optimal campaign performance.

# Google Ads – Boost Campaign Overview...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
Google 广告KOL视频投放上：

- 游戏类创作者拉流量快、点击多，适合快速曝光和转化。

- 科技类创作者观看率高，适合讲功能、教育用户、提升深度互动。 结论：根据目标灵活分配，既能扩大覆盖，也能保证转化质量。

---

| ParkerTheSlayer | Gaming | https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WIC6Aj9RU8I | 80% NEU, 20%CA & UK | Traffic | 1000 | 2 Weeks | FL_REDMAGIC_WW_EN_AWR_Traffic_DemandGen_11Air_KOL_Linked | 409,962 | 1,439 | 4,324 | 0.35 | 0.32 | 1.13 | 0.11 | $465 | 02/10 | 27/03 |

**[position: x:21, y:350]**
游戏类创作者（如 ParkerTheSlayer、BobbyPlays）：这类创作者通常拥有较低的观看率（View Rate），意味着其受众中完整观看视频的比例较低。但他们能够带来更高的点击量，因此在以快速引流和提升曝光为目标的活动中表现更为出色。他们的受众更倾向于快速行动，但对产品细节的深入了解相对较少。

科技类创作者（如 Chigz Tech、REDMAGIC）：相比之下，科技类创作者的观看率更高，说明其受众更专注、参与度更强。这使他们非常适合用于产品深度讲解类内容，能够帮助用户充分理解产品功能和优势。

总的来说，在游戏类与科技类创作者之间的选择应取决于活动目标：游戏类创作者更适合带来流量规模和即时转化，而科技类创作者则更擅长提升深度互动和用户教育。合理利用这一差异，有助于更高效地分配营销资源，从而最大化覆盖和效果。

**[position: x:37, y:342]**
Gaming Creators (e.g., ParkerTheSlayer, BobbyPlays): These creators tend to have lower View Rates, meaning a smaller percentage of their audience watches content to completion. However, they drive higher click volumes, making them highly effective for campaigns focused on quick traffic and awareness. Th
Tech Creators (e.g., Chigz Tech, REDMAGIC): In contrast, tech-focused creators achieve higher View Rates, suggesting their audiences are more engaged and attentive. This makes them ideal for campaigns aimed at in-depth product education, where it’s important that viewers fully understand features and benefits.
In short, choosing between gaming and tech creators depends on campaign objectives: gaming creators are great for volume and immediate action, while tech creators excel at driving meaningful engagement and educating the audience. Leveraging this distinction allows for a more strategic allocation of marketing resources to maximize both reach and impact.

# Social Ads – Boost Campaign Overview...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
这次 11 Air 在 Meta、TikTok 和 X 上主要做的是 Boost 投放，核心目标是放大曝光，所以 CTR 和转化偏低是预期内的。
素材是基于自然流量表现筛选的，优先放大已经验证过有互动的内容。
从结果看，不同视频时长表现都不错，这一点和 Google 偏好短视频不太一样，也说明我们的内容在社媒平台的适配性是OK的。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:129, y:8]**
Social Ads – Boost Campaign Overview

**[position: x:24, y:62]**
| Boost Platform | Link | Country | Actual Cost | Reach/ View | Impressions | CPM | Engagement Rate/ Result Rate | CTR |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Instagram | https://www.instagram.com/p/DUIhttGFHiK/ | NEU 50%, EU 50% | $300.00 | 166,954 | 386,072 | $0.78 | 12.12% | 0.16% |
| TikTok | https://www.tiktok.com/@redmagicglobal/video/7602579528485031182?lang=en | NEU 50%, EU 50% | $300.00 | 531,067 | 1,319,485 | $0.23 | 0.44% | 0.96% |
| X | https://x.com/redmagicgaming/status/2016844108002971706 | NEU 50%, EU 50% | $500.00 | 408,859 | 868,883 | $0.73 | 2.36% | 1.17% |
| X | https://x.com/redmagicgaming/status/2018625516467871921 | NEU 50%, EU 50% | $500.00 | 345,071 | 922,423 | $0.53 | 1.60% | 1.08% |
| Instagram | https://www.instagram.com/reel/DViikjHj9C7/?utm_source=ig_web_button_share_sheet | NEU 50%, EU 50% | $450.00 | 258,011 | 549,660 | $0.82 | 10.12% | 0.07% |
| Facebook | https://www.facebook.com/share/r/1GVUwfTCJB/ | NEU 50%, EU 50% | $450.00 | 245,925 | 621,415 | $0.72 | 12.54% | 0.27% |
| TikTok | https://www.tiktok.com/@redmagicglobal/video/7614088634978831630?is_from_webapp=1&sender_device=pc&web_id=7589868976810133005 | NEU 50%, EU 50% | $500.00 | 669,918 | 1,527,949 | $0.33 | 0.48% | 1.07% |

**[position: x:24, y:282]**
11 Air 在 Meta、TikTok 和 X 上均进行了 Boost 广告投放，并单独分配预算以最大化曝光量。这也是整体 CTR 和转化表现相对较低的原因之一。被用于 Boost 的视频素材主要基于其自然流量表现进行筛选，优先选择在原有渠道中已验证具备较强互动表现的内容。

所有 Boost 投放的素材均以提升曝光为核心目标，而非直接提升互动，因此 CTR 较低属于策略上的预期结果。这一策略与项目以最大化可见度为核心目标高度一致。

从结果来看，Boost 内容在不同视频时长下均表现良好，这一点与 Google 渠道偏好 1 分钟以内短视频有所不同。在 Meta、TikTok 和 X 等社交平台上，广告效果并不依赖于短视频形式，进一步验证了 Boost 视频内容在不同时长下的灵活性与有效性。

| https://www.tiktok.com/@igrowan/video/7607624610040925461?_r=1&_t=ZS-93zzFvwFoGW | Mexico | 1,498,658 | 27,174 | 71,298 | 1.81% | $0.53 | $800 |

**[position: x:2, y:383]**
The 11 Air campaign implemented boosted ads across Meta, TikTok, and X, with dedicated budget allocation to maximize impressions. This explains the lower CTR and result rates observed. Video selection for boosting was guided by organic performance indicators, prioritizing content that had already demonstrated strong engagement among existing channel audiences.

All boosted assets were optimized for impressions rather than direct engagement, which naturally resulted in lower CTR figures. This was an intentional strategy aligned with the campaign objective of maximizing visibility rather than interaction.

Boosted content proved effective regardless of video duration, in contrast to Google where shorter formats under one minute are preferred. Strong advertising outcomes on social platforms were not dependent on shorter formats, reinforcing the flexibility and effectiveness of boosted video content across Meta, TikTok, and X.

# Edited KOL Video Overview...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
从整体表现来看，本次投放已经验证出一批高效达人，包括 Deanwethers、Chigz Tech Review、Dr. Mike、Ogrobbytv 和 Dame Tech，在较低投放成本下实现了高ROAS，具备可持续放量的潜力。
平台层面，Meta 和 Google 均表现稳健，Meta在效率上更优，Google在规模上更有优势，整体来看两者都具备持续加码的空间。
内容策略上，本地化处理有效提升了用户相关性和互动基础，是本次表现稳定的重要原因之一。
中东区域目前以素材覆盖为主，后续会通过持续投放强化用户信任，同时逐步优化预算分配，进一步提升整体效率。

在 Meta 和 Google 两个平台上，11 Air campaign 持续实现强劲的 ROAS 表现。Meta 在预算使用效率上更优，而 Google 由于初期分配的预算占比更高，整体花费更大。尽管如此，两者的整体表现依然接近，均具备较强竞争力。
为进一步提升文化相关性和用户参与度，所有达人素材都全面本地化，包括视频剪辑和配套文案。
在中东地区，由于 KOL 视频数量超过预算承载能力，导致单个达人分配的预算较低。这些视频将在产品发布周期内持续投放，用于增强用户信任和品牌背书，同时在 ROAS 和 CTR 表现上保持稳定。

- Based on spend levels and ROAS outcomes, Deanwethers, Chigz Tech Review, Dr. Mike, Ogrobbytv, and Dame Tech emerged as the strongest performers. Each maintained ROAS above 150 with steady average spend of around $150.  


- Across Meta and Google, the 11 Air campaign continues to deliver strong ROAS. Meta shows greater efficiency in budget utilization, while Google’s higher spend reflects its larger share of the budget allocated from the start. Despite these differences, both platforms remain competitive with similar overall results.  


- English-language influencer videos consistently outperform localized versions. To strengthen cultural relevance and engagement, all influencer materials are being fully localized, including video edits and supporting copy.  


- In the ME region, the number of KOL videos exceeds the available budget, leading to lower spend per influencer. These videos will continue to be used throughout the product release period to reinforce trust and credibility, with performance remaining stable across both ROAS and CTR.

# Google Country Budget Allocation...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
从市场表现来看，墨西哥和阿联酋是整体效率最高的两个市场。
墨西哥ROAS做到47.31，虽然只用了8%的预算，但贡献了16%的收入，同时点击量也非常高，整体表现非常强劲。
阿联酋这边预算只有731美金，但ROAS也达到46.01，几乎接近墨西哥水平，说明小预算市场同样有很高的回报潜力。
非欧仍然是最大收入来源市场，贡献了45.9万美金左右，ROAS是20.75，整体表现稳定，但相对高效率市场来说还有优化空间。
巴西这边ROAS是10.24，相对偏低。不过它的曝光和点击都是全市场最高的，说明流量和互动没有问题，主要挑战是在转化效率上，这在新兴市场其实也比较常见。

---

**[position: x:29, y:269]**
高表现市场：墨西哥 & 阿联酋墨西哥是一个效率极高的市场，ROAS 达到 47.31。尽管其仅占总投放的 8%，却贡献了 16% 的总收入。同时，其互动表现也非常强劲，带来了 193,132 次点击，仅次于巴西。
阿联酋虽然预算规模较小（$731），但实现了 46.01 的 ROAS，接近墨西哥水平。这一效率几乎是美国或法国等主要市场的两倍，显示出其在小规模投放下依然具备极高的回报潜力。
核心市场：美国 美国仍然是最大的收入来源市场，贡献了 $459,691 的收入。其 ROAS 为 20.75，表现稳健，但相较部分欧洲市场略低，说明在当前规模下仍存在提升投放效率的空间。
表现较弱市场：巴西 巴西的投放效率最弱，ROAS 为 10.24，仅约为美国的一半。有意思的是，巴西在曝光（740 万+）和点击（227,804）上均排名第一，说明其具备较强的曝光和互动能力。但这些流量未能有效转化为高价值购买，反映出转化效率存在明显问题。这对像巴西这样的新兴市场是正常的.

**[position: x:36, y:268]**
Top Performers: Mexico & UAE

- Mexico stands out as a highly efficient market, delivering an exceptional ROAS of 47.31. Despite accounting for just 8% of total spend, it contributes 16% of overall revenue. It also generates strong engagement, with 193,132 clicks—second only to Brazil.

- UAE, while operating on a much smaller budget ($731), achieves a ROAS of 46.01. This is nearly double the efficiency seen in larger primary markets such as the US or France, highlighting its strong return potential even at low scale.
Core Market: United States

- The United States remains the largest revenue contributor, generating $459,691. Its ROAS of 20.75 is solid, but comparatively lower than several European markets, indicating room for efficiency improvements despite its scale.
Underperformer: Brazil

- Brazil shows the weakest efficiency, with a ROAS of 10.24—roughly half that of the US. Interestingly, it leads in both impressions (7.4M+) and clicks (227,804), suggesting strong visibility and engagement. However, this traffic is not translating into high-value conversions, pointing to a gap in conversion effectiveness. This is common for a new market like Brazil.

# Meta Country Budget Allocation...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
从营收结构来看，整体主要由 NEU 和墨西哥市场驱动，其次是欧洲核心市场。NEU在独立转化广告账户的加持下，贡献了最高收入。
墨西哥这边ROAS表现非常突出，效率很高，同时在11 Air项目中投放金额排名第三。
从整体营收体量来看，欧洲区域（包括德国、西班牙、法国和意大利）合并后仍然是贡献最高的市场，说明本地化策略和不同市场内容定制是有效的。
英国在欧洲核心市场和墨西哥之后，排名第四，表现也比较稳定。
加拿大因为营收表现不错，预算有一定提升，但整体仍然保持相对克制，和其他国家相比差异不大，主要是因为11 Air整体预算相比Pro和S Pro旗舰机型还是有限。
中东、巴西、澳大利亚和新加坡整体体量接近加拿大，但规模相对较小。其他欧洲国家则保持最低预算配置，日预算大约在10美金左右。

Revenue performance was led by NEU and MX, followed by the main EU countries. NEU contributed the highest earnings, combined by the NEU standalone conversion ad account.
MX delivered impressive ROAS, demonstrating strong efficiency. It ranked third in overall spending for the 11 Air campaign. While MX showed standout results, the EU region collectively generated the highest revenue when combining DE, ES, FR, and IT, reinforcing the impact of localized strategies and tailored content for diverse audiences.
The UK ranked fourth in revenue after the main EU markets and MX. Budget allocation for CA has been increased due to strong revenue performance, though spending remains intentionally conservative. The differences in CA’s budget compared to other countries are not significant, as the 11 Air budget is relatively modest when compared with flagship products such as the Pro and S Pro.
ME, BR, AU, and SG demonstrated solid sales rates similar to CA, though at lower levels. Other EU markets are maintained at the lowest budget allocation within the group, approximately $10 daily across all remaining EU countries.

  title: "[REDMAGIC] RM 11 Air Launch report"

# Perspective and Suggestions...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:129, y:8]**
Perspective and Suggestions

**[position: x:25, y:59]**
What improved.


- Tailored assets were developed for different audience segments, with traffic and conversion ads strategically deployed to maximize relevance and impact.

- Retargeting ads were introduced at the launch stage to ensure continued exposure among existing users, reinforcing campaign messaging and sustaining engagement.

- Efficiency Surge: Google ROAS increased from 17 to 32, nearly doubling revenue to $1.67M while maintaining a similar budget.

- Creative Excellence on Google: The "Render" video drove the highest volume, with a strong 3.91% CTR and 19 purchases.

- High-Potential Markets: Mexico and UAE led in efficiency, posting ROAS of 47.31 and 46.01, significantly surpassing the US in return per dollar spent. 
To improve. 

- Market Efficiency on Google: While the US generated the most revenue, its ROAS (20.75) lagged behind secondary markets such as Mexico (47.31).

- Creative Underperformance on Google: The "Lifestyle" video underdelivered, with a CPC of $0.49—over 10x higher than other video assets.

- Brazil Conversion Gap: Despite leading in impressions (7.4M+) and clicks, Brazil’s efficiency was the lowest (ROAS 10.24), indicating strong traffic but weak conversion. 

- Audience targeting was broadened to include smaller, niche segments supported by modest budgets. This expansion allowed the campaign to diversify its reach, engage audiences from different cultural and interest groups, and strengthen overall impact by capturing attention across a wider spectrum of consumer segments.

Next Steps.

- Creative Strategy: Build on the 11 Air’s success by using the "Render" video’s visual DNA as the foundation for the next product’s creative assets.

- Audience Targeting: Use the "Tech Geek" segment for early awareness (highest CTR at 2.83%) and the "Core Audience" for driving conversions.

- Creator Partnerships: Engage Tech Creators (e.g., Chigz Tech) for deep-funnel education and Gaming Creators for high-volume, rapid traffic.

- Audience performance will be monitored continuously to identify and deactivate assets or segments with limited effectiveness. The current strategy will be maintained to ensure comprehensive reach across all audiences through diverse ad content.

- KOL-driven ads will continue to be launched as partnerships whenever available, reinforcing trust and credibility.

- To sustain interest, new assets will be introduced regularly, using content sourced from organic social media channels to keep content new and relevant.


=====

---

  chunk_id: "1dYAL0iODb1x0sJpOQqMhcl0c5hni5OrPN-jl0-3K2x0_slide_70"
  file_url: "https://docs.google.com/presentation/d/1dYAL0iODb1x0sJpOQqMhcl0c5hni5OrPN-jl0-3K2x0/edit#slide=id.g3dd5ec61df8_0_204"
  title: "[REDMAGIC] RM 11 Air Launch report"
---

# Perspective and Suggestions...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
这一部分主要分三点来说:
首先是提升部分，这次我们通过人群定制素材和引流+转化组合投放，整体相关性和效率都有明显提升。同时在Launch阶段提前引入再营销，强化了用户持续触达。
效果上，Google ROAS从17提升到32，在预算基本持平的情况下，收入接近翻倍到1.67M。表现最好的素材是“Render”视频，CTR达到3.91%，也带来了19单转化。墨西哥和阿联酋是效率最高的市场，ROAS都在47左右。
但同时也有优化空间。美国虽然贡献最大收入，但ROAS相对较低；Lifestyle素材表现偏弱，CPC明显偏高；巴西则是典型的流量强但转化弱的问题。
最后是下一步优化方向，我们会延续Render这种视觉风格作为核心创意方向。在人群上，前期重点放Tech Geek，转化阶段聚焦Core Audience。同时结合KOL内容做素材扩展，持续优化人群和素材组合，提升整体效率和覆盖面。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:129, y:8]**
Perspective and Suggestions

**[position: x:25, y:59]**
What improved.

- 针对不同人群开发了定制化素材，并通过引流与转化广告的组合投放，最大化相关性与投放效果。

- 在Launch发布阶段提前引入再营销广告，确保对已有用户的持续触达，强化信息传递并维持用户参与度。

- 效率显著提升： Google ROAS 从 17 提升至 32，在预算基本持平的情况下，营收接近翻倍至 $1.67M。

- Google 素材表现突出： “Render” 视频成为核心驱动，CTR 达到 3.91%，并带来 19 笔购买。

- 高潜力市场： Google 墨西哥和阿联酋在效率上领先，ROAS 分别为 47.31 和 46.01，显著高于美国的单美元回报。

To improve. 

- Google 市场效率： 虽然美国贡献了最高收入，但其 ROAS（20.75）低于墨西哥（47.31）等次级市场。

- Google 创意表现： “Lifestyle” 视频表现较弱，CPC 较高达到 $0.49，是其他素材的 10 倍以上。

- 巴西转化问题： 尽管巴西在曝光（740 万+）和点击上领先，但 ROAS 仅为 10.24，说明流量强但转化效率不足。

- 人群拓展： 通过增加小众细分人群并配以较小预算，实现了更广泛的覆盖，增强了不同文化与兴趣群体的触达能力，从而提升整体投放效果。

Next Steps.

- 创意策略： 延续 11 Air 成功经验，以 “Render” 视频的视觉风格作为下一代产品创意的核心方向。

- 人群策略： 在前期认知阶段重点使用 “Tech Geek” 人群（CTR 最高 2.83%），在转化阶段重点投放 “Core Audience”。

- 达人合作：与科技类创作者（如 Chigz Tech）合作，强化中后链路的产品教育; 与游戏类创作者合作，获取大规模流量与快速转化

- 持续优化： 持续监测人群表现，及时下线效果不佳的素材或人群组合，同时保持多样化广告内容以覆盖更广泛受众。

- KOL 投放： 持续上线 KOL 内容作为广告素材，强化品牌信任与背书。

- 内容更新： 定期引入新素材，优先利用社交媒体自然流量内容进行二次创作，保持内容新鲜度与相关性。


=====

# PR Overview...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
本次评测中，科技类内容占比80%，相比10 Air的59%明显提升，另外游戏评测16%，综合评测7%。
11 Air整体平均评分达到9/10，高于10 Air，主要得益于更具竞争力的定价，带来更合理的用户预期。
开售后30天内评测发布率达到58%，节奏相对较快。
从分布来看，评测主要集中在欧洲及非欧市场，整体覆盖较为均衡。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:40, y:45]**
PR Overview

|---|---|
| 样机回转率 Recycle Rate | 89% (16 Units/18) |

**[position: x:25, y:101]**

# REDMAGIC 11 Air - Media Buy...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
媒体表现整体来看，BFM是最强贡献点，带来最高曝光、点击和收入，是核心驱动媒体。
Tom’s Hardware和GSMArena提供稳定的科技流量，属于主要流量来源。
NY Post偏品牌曝光，有高曝光但转化较弱。
Clubic、Journal du Geek和Gamestar整体表现均衡，提供稳定补充流量。
Droid Gamers和Gamezebo则在细分游戏人群中实现低成本触达。
Netzwelt表现相对一般，受节后流量波动影响较大。
整体来看，核心由BFM驱动，其它媒体做流量和人群补充。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:40, y:45]**
REDMAGIC 11 Air - Media Buy

**[position: x:84, y:118]**
Media Performance:-


- New York Post delivered 11,761 Page Views and 573 Clicks, supported by 1.15M Impressions at a CPM of $9.08. While CPC was on the higher side at $18.32, the placement provided strong premium exposure and generated $3,472 in sales, resulting in a ROAS of 0.33, reinforcing its value in driving visibility and direct conversion.

- Netzwelt generated 2,148 Page Views and 167 Clicks. While page views were comparatively lower, this is likely influenced by the post-holiday period, where audiences are typically less active and less inclined to shop. Despite this, it maintained steady delivery within the campaign mix.

- Journal du Geek achieved 10,232 Page Views and 788 Clicks, with an efficient CPV of €0.46 and CPC of €6.03, contributing consistent traffic and engagement.

- Clubic delivered 7,520 Page Views and 3,680 Clicks, supported by 300K Impressions. It performed well in driving engagement, with a strong CPC of €1.22 and CPV of €0.60.

- BFM stood out as a key performer, generating 3.62M Impressions at an outstanding CPM of €1.66, alongside 34,967 Page Views and 44,168 Clicks. It also drove €12,508.45 in sales, achieving a ROAS of 2.08, making it the most impactful partner in terms of scale, efficiency, and revenue contribution. 

- Droid Gamers/Gamezebo produced 13,505 Page Views with a highly competitive CPV of $0.11, effectively supporting targeted reach within niche gaming audiences. 

- GSMArena delivered 16,628 Page Views and 499 Clicks, maintaining efficient costs with a CPV of $0.15 and CPC of $5.01, while driving strong engagement among a tech-savvy audience.

- Tom’s Hardware emerged as a major traffic driver with 41,510 Page Views and 3,500 Clicks, supported by efficient CPV of €0.13 and CPC of €1.57, making it a key contributor to both reach and engagement. 

- Gamestar generated 16,153 page views and 2,163 clicks, delivering a balanced performance with solid engagement metrics across the board.

  title: "[REDMAGIC] RM 11 Air Launch report"

# REDMAGIC 11 Air - Media Buy...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
整体来看，本次是一个结构比较均衡的组合，未来可以重点放大BFM、Tom’s Hardware和NY Post，同时继续优化中腰部媒体的转化效率。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:40, y:45]**
REDMAGIC 11 Air - Media Buy

**[position: x:54, y:117]**
Key Insights:-


- Strong Traffic Drivers: Tom’s Hardware and BFM emerged as the primary traffic engines of the campaign, contributing the highest share of page views and clicks while maintaining highly efficient CPV and CPC—demonstrating their strength in scaling both reach and engagement simultaneously. 

- Scale Meets Efficiency: BFM clearly led in overall efficiency, combining high-volume impressions (3.62M) with one of the lowest CPMs and CPCs, while also delivering €12,508.45 in revenue (ROAS 1.19)—positioning it as the campaign’s most well-rounded performer across awareness, traffic, and conversion.

- Premium Visibility with Conversion Impact: New York Post delivered strong upper-funnel exposure backed by 1.15M impressions, while also generating $3,472 in sales. Despite higher CPC, it validated its role as a premium media partner capable of contributing to both brand visibility and direct revenue. 

- Efficient Engagement Contributors: Clubic and Gamestar stood out for their ability to convert impressions into clicks efficiently, delivering strong engagement metrics and reinforcing their value as mid-funnel performance drivers. 

- Niche Audience Effectiveness: Droid Gamers/Gamezebo and GSMArena provided highly targeted reach within gaming and tech audiences, achieving competitive CPV and consistent engagement—supporting qualified traffic and audience relevance. 

- Optimization Opportunity: Netzwelt performance was relatively softer in this instance, which may be influenced by timing factors such as the post-holiday period and shifting audience behavior. It’s important to note that evaluating a single article in isolation can be limiting, as results often vary based on seasonality and timing. For future activations, a multi-article approach is recommended to build consistency, better assess performance trends, and drive more stable, meaningful results over time.

- Balanced Media Mix: Overall, the campaign reflects a well-structured mix of premium, scale, and niche partners, successfully delivering on awareness, engagement, and revenue objectives. Moving forward, further efficiency gains can be unlocked by scaling high-performing platforms like BFM, New York Post, and Tom’s Hardware, while continuing to optimize premium placements for stronger conversion returns.

  title: "[REDMAGIC] RM 11 Air Launch report"
---

# Perspective and Suggestions-PR...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
整体来看，这次PR表现正面，头部媒体评分集中在8.8到9分，产品在游戏手机市场认可度较高。
媒体覆盖偏精准，主要集中在科技和游戏垂直领域，并且已经开始进入部分榜单，说明轻薄+性能的定位是被市场接受的。但节奏上发布偏慢，优质媒体排期和资源有限，整体扩展速度受限。
产品层面主要短板还是在相机和软件体验，同时泛用户市场对“游戏属性过强”的接受度还有差异。
整体结论是：定位清晰、口碑不错，但要扩大受众，需要继续补齐日常体验短板并提升传播广度。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:33, y:37]**
Perspective and Suggestions-PR

**[position: x:23, y:82]**
What Worked:-
- 头部媒体对 REDMAGIC 11 Air 的评分普遍在 8.8–9 分（满分 10 分）之间，表明其整体认可度较高，并在中高端游戏手机市场中具备较强竞争力。
- 媒体覆盖整体较为精准，主要集中在对游戏手机品类理解较深的科技与游戏垂直媒体，确保了评测更具专业性与客观性。同时，良好的运营支持（如及时沟通、清晰的产品定位资料及完整的媒体包）也帮助媒体更准确地传递产品信息，进一步提升了品牌可信度。
- 11 Air 已开始出现在“最佳轻薄游戏手机”“best lightweight gaming phone”和“高性价比游戏设备”“top budget gaming device”等榜单中，体现了市场对其“性能与便携性平衡”的认可。

What Not:-
- 由于季节性因素（相比 11 Pro），媒体发布仍存在一定延迟，但整体推进节奏保持稳定。大多数媒体在收到样机后均有反馈，少部分尚未回复，需要通过适度且不过于强势的方式持续跟进。
- 在游戏和科技垂直媒体中覆盖表现较强。但优质媒体资源相对有限，因此拓展节奏较缓。同时，样机循环（Recycling Program）运行顺畅，设备在媒体及其他渠道中实现了充分利用，但由于编辑排期积压和媒体资源有限，整体节奏相对较慢。
- 在产品层面，关于相机缺乏明显提升的批评仍在持续，进一步强化了其日常使用能力较弱的认知。软件体验依然是长期问题，包括 UI 不一致、Bug 以及本地化问题，亟需更系统性的优化。媒体对产品的期望也在提升，不仅仅是游戏性能，还包括更均衡的日常使用体验。一些媒体开始质疑，对于非重度游戏用户而言，如果核心手机功能表现不足，产品吸引力是否有限。
- 性能方面已得到充分认可，但部分媒体认为散热与游戏功能的强调略显过度，相较于普通用户的真实使用场景，差异化优势不够明显。整体来看，产品在游戏性能和设计上的定位依然清晰，但若希望扩大用户群体，必须在相机与软件体验上持续提升，以避免被视为过于小众的产品。

Suggestions & Learnings:-
媒体合作（Organic & Paid）：优先与头部、有影响力的媒体建立更深度的合作，而非单纯追求媒体数量。高信任度的媒体能够带来更强的品牌背书、更高质量的内容输出以及更一致的信息传递，这对于 REDMAGIC 11 Air 以游戏为核心的定位尤为重要。
最大化样机循环价值：对回收样机进行有策略的分配，覆盖潜在媒体、KOL 和联盟渠道。每一台设备都应最大化曝光、加强合作关系，并为未来项目或节点性活动保留灵活性。
策略性利用垂直及长尾渠道： 通过样机循环或联盟内容，合理利用中腰部及垂直媒体，在不影响品牌调性的前提下扩大覆盖，同时触达更精准的细分人群。
根据媒体节奏调整沟通策略： 媒体响应在不同阶段存在波动，应采用分批推进的方式，设定合理预期，并保持礼貌且持续的跟进，避免造成媒体疲劳的同时确保曝光。
淡季优化投放与内容准备： 在节奏较缓的阶段，重点优化媒体素材、沟通角度与信息表达。提前准备内容，有助于在集中发布期形成更强势的传播效果。
以洞察反哺产品与传播定位： 针对“性能与噱头认知差异”以及软件、相机等短板反馈，优化沟通策略。重点强化稳定游戏性能、OLED 屏幕、续航等实际优势，同时正视并回应弱项，以提升品牌可信度并拓展更广泛人群。
监测反馈，维护品牌声誉： 避免与过度负面的媒体合作，同时密切关注建设性反馈。针对反复出现的问题（如相机表现、系统体验、散热认知），应持续优化表达，并为后续产品定位与改进提供依据。


=====

  file_url: "https://docs.google.com/presentation/d/1dYAL0iODb1x0sJpOQqMhcl0c5hni5OrPN-jl0-3K2x0/edit#slide=id.g3dcdec81afc_0_97"
  title: "[REDMAGIC] RM 11 Air Launch report"

# Perspective and Suggestions-PR...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:33, y:37]**
Perspective and Suggestions-PR

**[position: x:50, y:82]**
What Worked:-


- Top media rated the REDMAGIC 11 Air between 8.8 and 9 out of 10, indicating strong overall approval and a competitive position within the mid-to-high-end gaming smartphone segment. Reviewers consistently highlighted its slim and lightweight design, improved ergonomics compared to bulkier gaming phones, and stable gaming performance powered by the Snapdragon chipset. The device’s advanced ICE Cooling System, high-refresh-rate AMOLED display, and responsive shoulder triggers were also frequently praised, reinforcing its positioning as a more accessible yet performance-driven gaming device.

- While reception was largely positive, several recurring criticisms emerged. The camera system was viewed as average, aligning with its gaming-first focus, while the software experience (REDMAGIC OS+Translation) was occasionally described as inconsistent, with minor UI quirks and translation issues. Some reviewers also noted that, despite the improved form factor, thermal performance under extended sessions—while stable—was not as aggressive as bulkier Pro models, representing a trade-off for portability.

- Media coverage remained well-targeted, focusing on credible tech and gaming-focused media that understand the category, helping ensure fair and context-aware evaluations. Strong operational support, such as timely responses, clear positioning materials, and detailed media kits, contributed to accurate and favorable reporting, reinforcing overall brand credibility.

- The 11 Air has also begun appearing in “best lightweight gaming phone” and “top budget gaming device” roundups, reflecting recognition of its balanced approach between performance and portability. These insights suggest that while it does not aim to outperform full-sized flagship gaming phones in every aspect, its design-forward approach, solid performance, and improved usability are resonating well with media and users, positioning it as a compelling alternative within the gaming smartphone category.

What Not:-


- Publication delays remain expected due to seasonal factors (compared to 11 Pro), but overall momentum is steady. Most media responded after receiving units, a few remain unresponsive, requiring careful, non-pushy follow-ups.

- Strong coverage within gaming and tech verticals. Limited pool of high-quality media means expansion should be paced to maintain flexibility for recycling and sustained coverage. However, Recycling Program running smoothly with full utilization of units across media and other channels, though pacing is gradual due to editorial backlog and limited bandwidth.

- Continued criticism on lack of meaningful camera improvements, reinforcing perception of weak everyday usability. Software experience remains a consistent issue, including UI inconsistencies, bugs, and localization, needs more serious attention. Increasing expectation for balanced daily usability, not just gaming. Some media question appeal for non-heavy gamers if core smartphone functions underperform.

- Performance is validated, but some media view cooling and gaming features as slightly overemphasized vs real-world differentiation for casual users. Strong positioning in gaming performance and design, but to expand appeal, camera and software improvements are critical to avoid being seen as too niche.


=====

  title: "[REDMAGIC] RM 11 Air Launch report"
---

# Perspective and Suggestions-PR...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:33, y:37]**
Perspective and Suggestions-PR

**[position: x:39, y:109]**
Suggestions & Learnings:-


- Media Partnerships (Organic & Paid): Prioritize deeper engagement with top-tier, influential media rather than maximizing media count. Trusted voices drive credibility, higher-quality coverage, and consistent messaging, which is especially valuable given REDMAGIC 11 Air’s gaming-first positioning.

- Maximize Impact from Recycled Units: Allocate recycled devices thoughtfully to potential media, KOLs, and affiliates. Each unit should maximize visibility, strengthen relationships, and retain flexibility for future campaigns or seasonal launches.

- Leverage Niche Channels Strategically: Lower-tier or niche media can be leveraged strategically via recycling programs or affiliate content, extending exposure without compromising brand prestige and reaching highly targeted audiences.

- Adapt Outreach to Media Timing: Media responsiveness fluctuates around peak periods. Stagger outreach, set realistic timelines, and maintain respectful persistence to avoid press fatigue while ensuring coverage.

- Optimize Campaigns During Slow Periods: Use slower periods to refine media assets, briefing angles, and messaging. Proactively preparing content during downtime ensures strong momentum during active publishing windows.

- Use Insights to Inform Product Positioning: Feedback on performance versus perceived gimmicks and software/camera weaknesses should guide communication. Emphasize tangible benefits like stable gaming performance, OLED display, and long battery life, while also addressing recurring weaker feedback to maintain credibility and broaden appeal beyond hardcore gamers.

- Monitor Feedback to Protect Brand Reputation: Avoid overly critical media while monitoring constructive feedback closely. Recurring points: camera quality, software quirks, and cooling perception, should be taken seriously to refine messaging and inform future positioning.


=====
