# Social Media: 11 Air vs 10 Air...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
整体来看，11 Air在各项指标上都显著优于10 Air。
覆盖提升81%，互动提升78%，粉丝增长达到37.8万，说明从曝光到沉淀的转化效率都有明显提升。
用户反馈也更积极，主要得益于更强的设计语言和Turbo Fan回归，强化了性能和游戏属性认知。
整体来看，这次表现更贴近REDMAGIC“性能+设计+游戏”的核心品牌定位，因此带来更好的市场结果。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:42, y:48]**
Social Media: 11 Air vs 10 Air

**[position: x:45, y:97]**
| REDMAGIC 11 Air |  |
|---|---|
| Reach | 89,521,054 |
| Engagement | 4,402,028 |
| Engagement Rate | 4.92% |
| Fans | 378,453 |

**[position: x:299, y:97]**
| REDMAGIC 10 Air |  |
|---|---|
| Reach | 49,436,268 |
| Engagement | 2,467,550 |
| Engagement Rate | 4% |
| Fans | +312,218 |

**[position: x:45, y:257]**
Insights:

- REDMAGIC 11 Air 在各项指标上均显著优于 10 Air，覆盖量提升 +81%（8950万 vs 4940万），互动量提升 +78%（440万 vs 247万）. 粉丝增长达到 37.8 万（对比 31.2 万），表明从曝光到用户沉淀的转化效果更佳。

- 用户对 11 Air 的反馈更加积极，这主要得益于其更强的 REDMAGIC 设计语言，相比 10 Air 较为单一的外观更具辨识度。 标志性 Turbo Fan 的回归（10 Air 中缺失）进一步强化了性能认知，并提升了社区吸引力。

- 整体来看，与 REDMAGIC 核心品牌基因（性能 + 设计 + 游戏属性）的更高一致性，直接带来了更出色的市场表现。


- REDMAGIC 11 Air significantly outperformed 10 Air across all metrics, with +81% reach (89.5M vs 49.4M) and +78% engagement (4.4M vs 2.47M)

- Audience growth increased to 378K new followers vs 312K, showing better conversion from reach to community

- Fans responded more positively to 11 Air, driven by a stronger REDMAGIC design identity compared to the 10 Air’s more monotone look

- The return of the iconic Turbo Fan (absent in 10 Air) reinforced performance credibility and boosted community appeal

- Overall, closer alignment with REDMAGIC’s core DNA (performance + design + gaming identity) directly translated into stronger results

# Social Media: Future suggestions/Next steps...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
下一步计划
1. 维持社区热度与正向讨论 持续维护论坛讨论活跃度，通过及时解答用户设备相关问题，强化品牌可靠性与透明度。 增加社区驱动活动（抽奖、投票、AMA、互动话题等），并在 Reddit、Discord、Facebook 等平台保持高频、可见的参与度。
2. 提升曝光与互动增长 持续产出趋势型与 meme 风格内容，扩大自然曝光与内容传播。 加强口碑内容放大，展示真实用户使用场景、设备搭配与体验分享，提升社交传播力。
3. 推动网站转化与购买意向 优化内容中的转化路径，增加清晰的 CTA，引导用户进入产品页及购买页面。 结合促销节点与关键营销时机进行内容发布，提升整体转化效率。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:42, y:46]**
Social Media: Future suggestions/Next steps

**[position: x:42, y:94]**
Next Steps:

  - 维持论坛热度和积极讨论度 Nurture community and gain positive messaging

    - Continue assisting users with device-related inquiries to reinforce reliability and transparency

    - Increase community-led activities (giveaways, polls, AMAs, engagement prompts)

    - Maintain active, visible participation across Reddit, Discord, and Facebook communities


  - 增加曝光和互动 Sustain Reach & Engagement Growth

    - Produce trend-driven and meme-style content to maximize organic reach and discovery

    - Amplify word-of-mouth content by showcasing satisfied customers, setups, and real-use scenarios


  - 为网站引流 Drive Conversion & Consideration

    - Integrate clearer CTAs linking to product pages and purchase opportunities

    - Align content timing with promotions and key sales moments to support conversion

# KOL Strategy & Performance Analysis Fastlane Side...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
这次 Fastlane KOL 项目一共合作了 53 位 KOL，在三大平台发布了 123 条内容。
整体带来 3,234 万次观看、97.6 万互动和 2.4 万点击，整体互动率是 3.6%，预算大概 5.3 万美金，执行率 79%。
从市场来看，主要集中在北美和欧洲，两边基本是核心覆盖。
内容结构上以科技类为主，游戏类和复合类做补充，整体兼顾了产品深度和游戏体验传播。
总结下来：覆盖广、结构均衡，在核心市场有不错的传播效果。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:128, y:7]**
KOL Strategy & Performance Analysis Fastlane Side

**[position: x:15, y:67]**
- Overall:

  - REDMAGIC 11 Air 项目共确认合作 53 位 KOL，目前已在 TikTok、YouTube 和 Instagram 等平台发布超过 123 条内容。

  - 累计数据表现为： 32,345,855 次观看、975,799 次点赞、53,193 条评论、44,014 次分享，以及 24,695 次点击，整体互动率为 3.60%。 总投入预算为 $52,855.55（发布率：79%）。

  - Detailed report: Link


- KOL 地域分布：

  - KOL 主要集中在美国，占比 22.6%；其次为墨西哥，占比 20.8%。西班牙和意大利各占 13.2%，同样占比高。其他主要市场包括法国（7.5%）、巴西（5.7%）和葡萄牙（3.8%）。加拿大和英国各占 1.9%。

  - 整体来看，北美占据主导地位，美国与墨西哥合计占比达 43.4%。与此同时，欧洲市场（英国、西班牙、葡萄牙、法国、意大利）合计贡献 39.6%，其中地中海区域表现尤为突出，互动表现强劲。


-   KOL 类型分布：

  - 科技类创作者占比最高，为 50.9%，确保了内容以性能与硬件深度解析为核心。

  - 游戏类 KOL 占比 28.3%，强化了体验与娱乐属性的传播。

  - 游戏+科技复合型创作者占比 18.9%，在性能与实际使用之间建立连接。

  - 娱乐类创作者占比 1.9%，为整体内容补充了生活方式与多元视角。

# KOL Strategy & Performance Analysis Fastlane Side...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:128, y:7]**
KOL Strategy & Performance Analysis Fastlane Side

**[position: x:14, y:50]**
- Overall:

  - 53 KOLs were confirmed for the REDMAGIC 11 Air campaign, resulting so far in over 123 videos across multiple platforms, including TikTok, YouTube, and Instagram.

  - The content collectively generated approximately:

    - 32,345,855 views, 975,799 likes, 53,193 comments, and 44,014 shares, with a total of 24,695 clicks.

    - This translated to an overall engagement rate of 3.60%.

    - Total budget spent: $52,855.55 (release rate: 79%)

  - Detailed report: Link


- Geographical Distribution of KOLs:

  - The largest share of KOLs is concentrated in the United States, which accounts for 22.6% of the total pool. Mexico follows as the second-largest contributor at 20.8%, while Spain and Italy both maintain a significant presence at 13.2% each. Other notable contributors include France at 7.5%, Brazil at 5.7%, and Portugal at 3.8%. The remaining distribution is comprised of several key markets, including Canada and the United Kingdom, each representing 1.9%.

    - This distribution highlights a dominant North American presence, with the U.S. and Mexico together making up 43.4% of the KOL pool. This is complemented by a robust and diverse European footprint, which accounts for 39.6% of the total reach across the UK, Spain, Portugal, France, and Italy, led primarily by strong engagement in the Mediterranean markets.


- KOL Distribution by Type

  - Tech creators lead the distribution at 50.9%, ensuring a primary focus on technical performance and hardware deep-dives. Gaming KOLs represent the second-largest segment at 28.3%, adding a crucial layer of experiential and entertainment-driven reach. The remaining pool is comprised of Gaming/Tech specialists at 18.9%, providing a bridge between high-performance utility and practical use, while the Entertainment niche at 1.9% rounds out the campaign with a unique lifestyle perspective.

# KOL Strategy & Performance Analysis Fastlane Side...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
11 Air 本次合作规模明显扩大，KOL 数量提升约 47%，内容量接近翻倍，观看量也实现超过 100% 增长，整体覆盖面显著提升。
从效率来看，10 Air 的互动率和点击表现更高。但需要说明的是，11 Air 进行了更大规模的 boost 投放，主要用于扩大曝光，因此在一定程度上会影响互动等效率指标，其核心目标仍是最大化覆盖与曝光。
传播结构上，11 Air 覆盖更全球化，北美与欧洲分布更均衡；而 10 Air 更集中在墨西哥市场，整体表现更偏深度。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:126, y:5]**
KOL Strategy & Performance Analysis Fastlane Side

**[position: x:10, y:49]**
对比：REDMAGIC 10 Air vs REDMAGIC 11 Air
REDMAGIC 11 Air 相较于 10 Air，在红人合作规模、内容产出和整体曝光上实现了明显提升。但在效率指标（如互动率和引流能力）方面，10 Air 仍表现更优。

创作者与内容规模： REDMAGIC 11 Air：53 位 KOL｜123+ 条视频 REDMAGIC 10 Air：36 位 KOL｜64+ 条视频
11 Air 红人数量提升 +47%，内容产出提升 +92%，体现出更广泛的红人覆盖和更强的内容扩展能力。

曝光与互动： REDMAGIC 11 Air：3235 万观看｜3.60% 互动率 REDMAGIC 10 Air：1518 万观看｜8.48% 互动率
11 Air 的观看量提升 +113%（翻倍增长），但 10 Air 的互动率显著更高，说明其内容对用户的吸引力和粘性更强。

互动与传播： 点赞：97.6 万（11 Air）vs 104 万（10 Air） 评论：5.3 万（11 Air）vs 9.8 万（10 Air） 分享：4.4 万（11 Air）vs 3.9 万（10 Air）→ 提升 +12%
在更大曝光基数下，11 Air 仍保持较强的分享能力，分享量提升 12%，显示其在新市场中的传播潜力更高。
 点击量：24,695（11 Air）vs 33,638（10 Air）

地域分布与市场拓展： 11 Air 展现出更明显的全球化扩张：

- 美国（22.6%）、墨西哥（20.8%）为核心市场

- 欧洲市场显著拓展（西班牙、意大利各 13.2%）
相比之下，10 Air 更集中于墨西哥（32.4%），整体更偏向单一市场的深度表现。

总结： 11 Air 实现了规模与曝光的全面提升，而 10 Air 在用户粘性和转化效率上表现更优。


=====

# KOL Strategy & Performance Analysis Fastlane Side...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:126, y:5]**
KOL Strategy & Performance Analysis Fastlane Side

**[position: x:10, y:42]**
- Comparison: REDMAGIC 10 Air vs REDMAGIC 11 Air


  - The REDMAGIC 11 Air campaign marked a clear scale-up in creator partnerships, content volume, and overall reach compared to the 10 Air launch. While reach and visibility significantly improved, efficiency metrics (engagement rate and traffic generation) remained stronger for the 10 Air.


  - Creator & Content Scale: 

    - REDMAGIC 11 Air: 53 KOLs | 123+ videos

    - REDMAGIC 10 Air: 36 KOLs | 64+ videos.

  - The 11 Air campaign activated +47% more creators and generated +92% more content, reflecting a substantially broader creator footprint and stronger adoption.


  - Audience Reach & Engagement:

    - REDMAGIC 11 Air: 32.35M views | 3.60% engagement rate

    - REDMAGIC 10 Air: 15.18M views | 8.48% engagement rate.

  - 11 Air views increased by +113% (more than double). However, the 10 Air maintained a significantly higher engagement rate, suggesting that while the 11 Air reached a broader audience, the 10 Air content was more "sticky" with its viewers.


  - Interactions & Virality:

    - Likes: 975.8K (11 Air) vs 1.04M (10 Air)

    - Comments: 53.2K (11 Air) vs 98.2K (10 Air)

    - Shares: 44.0K (11 Air) vs 39.2K (10 Air) → +12% increase

  - While reaching a broader, more diverse audience, the 11 Air maintained strong shareability, with total shares increasing by 12%, signaling higher viral potential in new markets.

  - Traffic: 

    - 24,695 (11 Air) vs 33,638 (10 Air)


  - Geographical Reach & Market Expansion:

    - The 11 Air campaign demonstrated a clear international expansion strategy:

      - Strong presence in the US (22.6%) and Mexico (20.8%)

      - Expanded footprint in Europe (Spain & Italy at 13.2% each)

    - In contrast, the 10 Air campaign was more concentrated in Mexico (32.4%), indicating a more localized performance focus.

  title: "[REDMAGIC] RM 11 Air Launch report"
---

# Perspective and suggestions-KOLs...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
REDMAGIC 11 Air 这次活动整体曝光明显提升，截至 04.03 已合作 53 位 KOL，产出 123+ 条视频，相比以往 Air 系列有明显增长。TikTok、YouTube 和 Instagram 的组合投放，也有效提升了整体品牌认知。
亮点方面： 首先，内容复用效率不错，一些素材在付费投放中表现很好，比如 deanwethers 在美国的素材，ROAS 达到 140，带来 27 单；techjee 在意大利也带来 15 单，成本效率不错。 其次，整体规模几乎翻倍，覆盖更多科技和游戏人群。 同时，全球布局更均衡，北美和欧洲的覆盖都更稳定，不再过度集中单一市场。
挑战方面： 主要是传播爆点不足。虽然曝光提升，但因为整体设计偏简约，没有像 Pro 系列那样形成强话题传播。 另外互动深度也弱于 10 Air，缺少类似 newsec 合作那种高互动活动（当时 ER 16.7%）。
下一步： 接下来重点会放在三点：继续扩大 KOL 覆盖、强化内容二次投放利用，以及做 1–2 个高互动的联合活动，提升用户参与和社区热度。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:126, y:5]**
Perspective and suggestions-KOLs

**[position: x:18, y:47]**
REDMAGIC 11 Air 活动成功扩展了其曝光量，至 04.03 为止，吸引了 53 位 KOL，并生成了 123+ 条视频。这代表着与以往的“Air”系列发布相比，创作者合作关系实现了显著扩大。我们的 TikTok、YouTube 和 Instagram 策略持续提高了品牌认知度。

亮点：

- 内容使用： 某些 11 Air 资产显示红人渠道外的跨渠道潜力。例如：

  - deanwethers（美国）的视频被重新用于付费广告，效率颇高：ROAS 达 140，带来 27 笔购买（CTR 为 3%）。

  - techjee_（意大利）：带来了 15 笔购买，花费为 124 美元。

- 创作者规模与扩展： 成功将内容产出几乎翻倍，相较于 10 Air，确保品牌在更多元的科技和游戏社区中曝光。

- 全球覆盖： 成功在北美和欧洲建立了主导地位，逐步摆脱了以往过于集中于单一地区的局面。
难点：

- 设计传播性对比： 11 Air 的简约设计未能触发与更高阶型号相同的“病毒式”传播效应。相比之下，REDMAGIC 11 Pro 因其大胆的外观设计和明显的液冷系统，获得了 75,904,032 次观看，互动率为 5.72%。

- 互动深度： 尽管观看量较高，11 Air 缺乏 10 Air 高峰期那样的“病毒性吸引力”。

- 案例研究（REDMAGIC 10 Air）： 与 newsec 联合创作的 Instagram 活动促销带来了 16.7% 的互动率。复制这一层次的社区“热度”对于 11 Air 的下一阶段至关重要。

下一步/建议：

- KOL 扩展： 持续增加 KOL 合作，保持增长势头，接触未触及的 KOL 类别和市场，扩展创作者池。

- 内容再利用： 最大化投资回报率，通过社交媒体和付费广告使用 KOL 创作内容。

- 复制 "newsec 风格" 的互动： 11 Air 缺乏像 10 Air 通过 newsec 活动所实现的高强度互动（16.7% ER，605K 观看量 / 101K 互动）。 行动： 在 11 Air 下一阶段要求 1-2 个高层次的联合创作赠品活动，将被动观众转化为积极评论者和追随者。


=====

# Perspective and suggestions-KOLs...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:126, y:5]**
Perspective and suggestions-KOLs

**[position: x:18, y:47]**
Overall：

- The REDMAGIC 11 Air campaign successfully expanded its reach, engaging 53 KOLs and generating 123+ videos till 04.03. This represents a significant scaling of creator partnerships compared to previous "Air" series launches. Our strategy across TikTok, YouTube, and Instagram continued to yield high brand awareness. 

- Highlights:

  - Content Usage: Certain 11 Air assets showed commercial potential. Two examples:

    - deanwethers’s video (US), which was repurposed for paid ads with efficiency: 140 ROAS with 27 purchases (3% CTR).

    - techjee_'s video (IT): 15 purchases with $124 USD spent.

  - Creator Scale & Expansion: Successfully nearly doubled content output compared to the 10 Air, ensuring the brand was visible across more diverse tech and gaming communities.

  - Global Reach: Successfully established a dominant North American and European footprint, moving away from regional concentration.

- Roadblocks:

  - Design Virality Comparison: The clean, minimalist design of the REDMAGIC 11 Air did not trigger the same organic "viral" explosion seen in higher-tier models. By comparison, the REDMAGIC 11 Pro received 75,904,032 views with a 5.72% engagement rate, largely driven by its bold aesthetic and visible liquid cooling system.

  - Engagement Depth: While views were high, the 11 Air lacked a "viral hook" compared to the 10 Air’s peak activations. 

    - Case Study (REDMAGIC 10 Air): The newsec co-created Instagram giveaway delivered a massive 16.7% Engagement Rate.. Replicating this level of community "buzz" is highly recommended for the next phase of the 11 Air.

- Next Steps / Recommendations:

  - KOL Expansion: Continue onboarding new KOLs to maintain momentum and reach untapped KOLs’ categories and markets, expanding our pool.

  - Content Repurposing: Maximize ROI by using KOL-generated content across social media and paid ads.

  - Replicate "newsec-style" Engagement: The 11 Air lacks the high-intensity interaction seen in the 10 Air’s 16.7% ER (605k views / 101k interactions) from the newsec giveaway.

    - Action: Mandate 1-2 high-tier co-created giveaways for the next phase of the 11 Air to convert passive viewers into active commenters and followers.


=====

# Affiliate Performance...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
从流量与销售来看：
1. 流量（Sessions）最高的国家分别为非欧、法国、墨西哥、德国、荷兰和巴西，流量主要由 Ads、Sub-affiliates 及内容类联盟贡献。
2. 销售（Sales）最高的国家分别为非欧、墨西哥、法国和巴西，主要由 Ads、优惠券/Deals 以及返现类联盟驱动。
3. 关键洞察

- 非欧：仍为绝对核心市场，贡献超35%流量及近40%销售，需重点维护稳定表现。

- 法国：欧洲领先市场，且是主要欧洲国家中唯一实现增长的市场，显示更强的购买力。

- 墨西哥 & 巴西：从“高流量低转化”转为“高销售贡献”，进入前五销售市场，体现出明显的价格敏感与增长潜力。
总结: 整体联盟营销结构更健康，核心市场稳固，新兴市场转化能力提升明显。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:42, y:46]**
Affiliate Performance
30 DAYS AFTER OPEN SALES (Data from GA4)

**[position: x:327, y:100]**
Sessions:
高流量国家 Top sessions countries: 
Region: US, FR, MX, DE, NL, BR
Affiliates: Ads, Sub-affiliates and Content type affiliates contributed the most

Sales:高销量国家 Top sales countries: 
Country: US, MX, FR, BR
Affiliates: Ads, Coupon/Deals and Cashback affiliates contributed the most

美国：绝对主导地位 美国市场仍然占据主导地位，贡献超过35%的流量和接近40%的销售额。需要集中关注和维护，同时通过减少INTL站点链接重定向的影响来维持流量和销售的表现。
法国：引领欧洲，受价值偏好驱动 法国继续在欧洲市场领先，在流量和订单量上均排名欧洲国家首位。与11 Pro相比，法国是所有主要欧洲市场（法国、西班牙、意大利、德国）中唯一呈现增长份额的国家。这表明，高价值产品可能对法国用户具有更强的吸引力，推动了其市场的增长。
拉丁美洲：墨西哥和巴西：受价值敏感性驱动的潜力增长 与之前的时期不同，墨西哥和巴西市场不再呈现高流量但低转化的模式。这两个国家现在均排名前五的销售份额，反映出它们对价格的敏感性和对高价值产品的偏好。这显示了这两个市场强大的潜力。

# Affiliate Performance...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:42, y:46]**
Affiliate Performance
30 DAYS AFTER OPEN SALES (Data from GA4)

**[position: x:327, y:100]**
Sessions:
高流量国家 Top sessions countries: 
Region: US, FR, MX, DE, NL, BR
Affiliates: Ads, Sub-affiliates and Content type affiliates contributed the most

Sales:高销量国家 Top sales countries: 
Country: US, MX, FR, BR
Affiliates: Ads, Coupon/Deals and Cashback affiliates contributed the most


United States: Absolute Dominance
The U.S. market continues to hold a dominant position, contributing over 35% of traffic and nearly 40% of sales. It requires focused attention and maintenance, while mitigating the impact of link redirection in order to sustain both traffic and sales performance.

France: Leading Europe, Driven by Value Preference
France continues to lead the European market, ranking highest among European countries in both traffic and order volume. Compared with the 11 Pro, France is the only country among the key European markets (France, Spain, Italy, Germany) to show an increasing share. This indicates that high‑value products may exert stronger appeal among French users, contributing to its growth.


Latin America: Mexico and Brazil: Rising Potential Driven by Value Sensitivity
Unlike previous periods, the markets in Mexico and Brazil no longer show the pattern of high traffic but low conversion. Both countries now rank among the top five in sales share, reflecting their sensitivity to pricing and preference for high‑value products. This demonstrates the strong potential of these two markets.

  title: "[REDMAGIC] RM 11 Air Launch report"

# Perspective and suggestions...

## 🎤 核心摘要与演讲者备注 (Speaker Notes)
整体来看，联盟佣金率下降了22%，主要是我们针对不同联盟类型做了差异化调整，尤其是Awin的折扣类联盟佣金下调，但没有影响整体销售，反而让佣金结构更健康。
目前联盟占比控制在14.68%，基本稳定在15%以下，整体可控。
同时，我们也优化了数据追踪和归因方式，让联盟数据分析更精细、更准确，提升了后续决策支持能力。
下一步，会继续清理旧的、未分类的联盟资源，同时探索新的归因方式，重点向内容创作者型联盟转型，提升合作效率和内容带动能力。

---

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:42, y:48]**
Perspective and suggestions

**[position: x:37, y:96]**
What improved.
整体来说，联盟佣金率再次下降了22%。在2026年，我们根据不同类型的联盟进一步调整了佣金率。具体来说，针对在Awin上以折扣为主的联盟，他们产生了大量的订单，因此佣金率被降低。这一调整显著降低了整体佣金率，但没有影响到总销售结果，从而实现了更合理的佣金分配。
联盟占比为14.68%。在调整了订单审核规则后，我们逐步将联盟占比控制在15%以下。除了主要的促销期间，联盟渠道可能会导致临时增加，整体占比现在已大致稳定并得到控制。
通过跟踪、调试和内部技术措施，我们已实现了更加精细的联盟数据分析。这基本消除了以前仅依赖时间段过滤来汇总推广数据的限制，解决了长期存在的准确性不足问题。提高的精确度现在为未来决策提供了更强的数据支持。

To improve.
审查遗留的联盟资源 在一些较旧或功能不全的平台上，仍有大量未标记和未分类的联盟资源。这些需要进行系统化审查和整理，以便将来根据联盟类型调整佣金时能够覆盖所有平台。这将有助于减少佣金成本，并更有效地引导资源分配。

Next Steps.
我们正在探索新的联盟归因方法，转向以内容创作者为重点。这一战略旨在通过联盟计划增加内容创作者的合作意愿，并提高内容创作者在联盟组合中的整体占比


=====

chunk_metadata:
  chunk_id: "1dYAL0iODb1x0sJpOQqMhcl0c5hni5OrPN-jl0-3K2x0_slide_107"
  file_url: "https://docs.google.com/presentation/d/1dYAL0iODb1x0sJpOQqMhcl0c5hni5OrPN-jl0-3K2x0/edit#slide=id.g3dd5ec61df8_0_320"
  title: "[REDMAGIC] RM 11 Air Launch report"
---

# Perspective and suggestions...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:42, y:48]**
Perspective and suggestions

**[position: x:49, y:96]**
What improved.

- The overall affiliate commission rate has once again decreased by 22%. In 2026, we further adjusted commissions based on different types of affiliates. Specifically, commission rates for discount‑focused affiliates on Awin, who generate a large volume of orders, were reduced. This adjustment significantly lowered the overall commission rate without impacting total sales results, leading to a more reasonable allocation of commissions.


- Affiliate share stands at 14.68%. Following adjustments to the order review rules, we have gradually brought the affiliate share under 15%. Apart from major promotional periods, where reliance on affiliate channels may cause temporary increases, the share is now largely stable and under control.


- Through tracking, debugging, and internal technical measures, we have achieved more refined analysis of affiliate data. This has essentially eliminated the previous limitation of relying solely on time‑period filters to compile promotion data, resolving the long‑standing issue of insufficient accuracy. The improved precision now provides stronger data support for future decision‑making.

To improve.
Review of Legacy Affiliate Resources
On some older or less functional platforms, a large number of affiliate resources remain untagged and unclassified. These need to be systematically reviewed and organized, so that future commission adjustments by affiliate type can be extended across all platforms. This will help reduce commission costs and guide resource allocation more effectively.

Next Steps.

- We are exploring new approaches to affiliate attribution, shifting the focus toward content creators. This strategy aims to increase their willingness to collaborate with us through affiliate programs and raise the overall share of content creators within the affiliate mix.


=====

# EDM-Overview (Before Open Sales) Data Range: 11 Ai...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:43, y:46]**
EDM-Overview (Before Open Sales) Data Range: 11 Air 2026.01.20 - 2026.02.10 VS 10 Air 2025.04.16 - 2025.05.06

**[position: x:43, y:89]**
|  | Session | New Users | New Subscribers (Teasing~Launch) | Avg Open Rate | Avg Click Rate |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| REDMAGIC 11 Air | 70,997 | 22,521 | 47011 | 17.42% | 0.78% |
| Compare | 7.94%⬆ | 47.08%⬆ | 32.65%⬆ | 83%⬆ | 69%⬆ |
| REDMAGIC 10 Air | 65,776 | 15,312 | 35,439 | 9.5% | 0.46% |

**[position: x:630, y:251]**
Source from:GA4＆Mailchimp

**[position: x:50, y:265]**
Insight & analysis

- The REDMAGIC 11 Air demonstrates a consistent upward trend in user engagement during the pre-sale phase. Total sessions increased by 7.94%, while New Users and New Subscribers grew by 47.08% and 32.65% respectively, indicating an expanded brand reach and effective traffic acquisition. Notably, the 83% increase in Open Rate and 69% growth in Click Rate reflect a substantial improvement in interaction efficiency. This optimization is primarily attributed to the migration from Mailchimp to Klaviyo and the strategic removal of inactive users. By focusing on a more active audience, the 11 Air has successfully captured higher brand interest and driven stronger individual participation compared to the previous generation.

- REDMAGIC 11 Air 的预热数据在各项核心指标上均呈现增长态势。总访问量提升了 7.94%，同时新用户数与新订阅人数分别增长了 47.08% 和 32.65%，显示出品牌触达范围的进一步扩大与有效的用户引流。值得注意的是，邮件打开率（+83%）与点击率（+69%）的大幅提升，反映了用户互动效率的显著优化。这一增长主要得益于邮件平台从 Mailchimp 迁移至 Klaviyo，并通过剔除不活跃用户优化了数据库质量。在相似的统计周期内，这些策略确保了 11 Air 成功触达兴趣更浓厚的受众群体，实现了更高质量的用户互动。


=====

根据数据，参与度在预热和发布阶段保持稳定，11 Air的EBO最后召集和忠诚优惠邮件序列实现了最高的互动质量。值得注意的是，忠诚优惠邮件的打开率达到了高峰（在阿联酋最高为30.08%），点击率也创下新高（在英国和加拿大最高为2.8%），这表明老用户的兴趣非常强烈，也和机制优化有关。

Data Range: 11 Air 2026.02.10 - 2026.03.11 VS 10 Air 2025.05.06 - 2025.06.04

**[position: x:552, y:106]**
Source from:GA4＆Mailchimp＆Klaviyo

**[position: x:85, y:143]**
|  | Session | New Users | Returning Users | Revenue |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| REDMAGIC 11 Air | 72,894 | 29,077 | 11,515 | $89,533.29 |
| Compare | -10.78% | 56.5%⬆ | 6.22%↓ | 185.77%⬆ |
| REDMAGIC 10 Air | 81,698 | 18,580 | 12,279 | $31,330.08 |

**[position: x:43, y:282]**
强劲的新用户增长和收入激增：与10 Air发布期相比，总会话数下降了10.78%。这一下降主要归因于10 Air阶段的极高流量基数，当时正好有Cooler 6系列的发布、毕业季促销以及REDMAGIC 10S Pro预热活动的推动。尽管整体流量较低，但新用户增长了56.5%，这表明11 Air在吸引新客户和拓展品牌市场份额方面具备强大的竞争吸引力。

显著的收入表现和转化效率：11 Air发布实现了185.77%的收入增长。虽然回访用户有所下降（-6.22%），但新用户的显著增长大大抵消了这一影响，导致转化价值大幅提升。这一变化表明，11 Air有效地捕获了高意向买家。

Strong New User Growth and Revenue Surge：Total sessions saw a 10.78% dip compared to the 10 Air launch period. This decline is primarily attributed to the exceptionally high traffic baseline during the 10 Air phase, which benefited from the simultaneous launch of the Cooler 6 Series, the Graduation Sale, and the REDMAGIC 10S Pro teasing campaign. Despite the lower overall traffic, New Users surged by 56.5%, demonstrating the 11 Air’s strong competitive appeal in acquiring new customers and expanding the brand's market reach. 56.5%↑

Significant Revenue Performance and Conversion Efficiency：The 11 Air launch achieved a remarkable revenue increase of 185.77%. While Returning Users saw a minor decline of 6.22%, the substantial growth in new user acquisition significantly offset this, resulting in a much higher conversion value. This shift indicates that the 11 Air effectively captured high-intent buyers.

# EDM (After Open Sales)...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:42, y:46]**
EDM (After Open Sales)

**[position: x:27, y:348]**
与REDMAGIC 11 Air开售同步，我们转向使用Klaviyo，并执行了战略性的邮件列表清理，只迁移了活跃用户。这一转变显著提升了整体参与度。在开售后，我们实施了以产品教育为核心的活动，重点介绍了REDMAGIC 11 Air的主要卖点，包括电池、屏幕和设计。性能数据显示，用户对产品设计最感兴趣，尤其是通过GIF横幅展示时；因此，我们建议在未来的营销活动中增加此类以设计为主的内容的频率。
Coinciding with the REDMAGIC 11 Air Open Sale, we transitioned to Klaviyo and executed a strategic list cleaning to migrate only active users. This transition significantly enhanced overall engagement. 

Following the open sale, we implemented product education campaigns centered on the REDMAGIC 11 Air's key selling points, including battery, screen, and design. Performance data indicates that users are most interested in product design, particularly when showcased via GIF banners; therefore, we recommend increasing the frequency of such design-centric content in future campaigns.

# EDM: Future suggestions/Next steps...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:42, y:46]**
EDM: Future suggestions/Next steps

**[position: x:38, y:109]**
To Improve

- Hybrid Content Structure:尝试采取图文分离形式提高AI抓取度 Transition from image-heavy templates to a text-and-image separated (Hybrid) layout. This allows AI-driven inbox tools (like Gemini in Gmail) to better crawl and prioritize email content, improving deliverability and visibility.

- Visual & Educational Engagement: 产品预热和开售期间持续的产品教育推送 Continue integrating GIFs and Product Education content. The success of the 11 Air launch confirms that these elements significantly drive click-through rates and high-intent conversions.

- Strategic A/B Testing: A/B Test 确定最佳发送时间和标题类型 Perform consistent testing on the entire database to determine optimal send times and the best-performing personalized subject line types.
Next Step
潜在用户池激活：实施针对尚未购买的新订阅者的专门自动邮件，专注于混合内容策略，以确保更高的邮箱到达率。

- Potential Pool Activation: Implement a dedicated outreach flow targeting the new subscribers who have not yet purchased, focusing on the hybrid content strategy to ensure higher inbox placement.
本地化用户生成内容（UGC）整合：通过整合本地化UGC，将美国地区成功的产品教育模式在全球范围内推广，以维持11 Air的销售势头。

- Localized UGC Integration: Scale the successful US-region Product Education model globally by integrating localized UGC to sustain the 11 Air’s sales momentum.

**[position: x:28, y:353]**
- 与 REDMAGIC 10 Air 相比，REDMAGIC 11 Air 的表现增长显著：博客内容总点击量提升了约 96%（达到 65.2K），曝光量增长了约 237%（达到 4.01M），博客内容平均排名也从 第 11 位 提升至 第 5.7 位。数据变化表明市场对 11 Air 的搜索需求和关注度大幅提高，整体流量规模和内容曝光率均有明显增长，反映出消费者对新一代 Air 系列产品展现出了更浓厚的兴趣。

- Compared to the REDMAGIC 10 Air, the REDMAGIC 11 Air showed significant growth: total blog clicks increased by approximately 96% (reaching 65.2K), while impressions grew by about 237% (reaching 4.01M). Additionally, the average ranking for blog content improved from 11 to 5.7. These metrics indicate a substantial rise in search demand and market visibility for the 11 Air, with a marked increase in overall traffic scale and content reach, reflecting much stronger consumer interest in this new generation of the Air series.

- 内容分析： 在新品发布阶段， 博客数据表明 产品信息类 (Product Information) 内容最能吸引用户，平均每篇文章产生 329 次访问，互动率位居首位。尽管 Game Space 相关内容的发布量更大，但产品教育 (Product Education) 和专注于参数解析的文章在单篇引流能力上持续领先。这表明在新品首发这一关键时期，受众对详尽的硬件参数和深度产品信息的关注度最高。


- During the new product launch phase, blog performance data indicates that Product Information is the most compelling category, generating the highest engagement with an average of 329 sessions per post. While Game Space content has a higher volume, it is the Product Education and spec-heavy articles that consistently drive superior traffic per post. This suggests that during this critical launch period, the audience is most effectively captivated by detailed hardware specifications and in-depth product data.


- 后续行动： 通过发布更多 Game Space 实操话题及常见问题解答来优化 SEO。同时，完善图片 Alt 文本和结构化数据以提升页面权重和入口流量。


- Next Steps: Boost SEO rankings by expanding Game Space content and addressing common user FAQs sourced from support inquiries. Additionally, optimize the blog’s accessibility by refining image alt text and structured data.

# Social Listening - Comments Analysis (KOLs)...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:132, y:3]**
Social Listening - Comments Analysis (KOLs)

**[position: x:22, y:50]**
整体情绪：积极 - 用户反应热烈，重点赞扬设备的性能、设计和性价比，偶尔提到一些小的妥协，如相机质量或软件更新。
优点： 性能与规格： 设备普遍被认为强大，能够应对游戏和高负荷使用。 用户强调它提供了“旗舰级的性能，价格却是中端价位”。 性价比： 是积极情绪的主要驱动因素。 许多评论强调，凭借这个价格，规格非常令人印象深刻，并且与苹果和三星等更贵品牌做对比。 显示与设计： 干净的全面屏（无开孔）和整体美学设计受到高度赞扬。用户形容其“超干净”、“现代”，在视觉上与高端设备竞争力十足。 游戏吸引力： 对寻找专用游戏设备的玩家有很强的吸引力。 一些用户明确表示会购买或考虑购买作为一款以游戏为主的备用手机。 电池与硬件配置： 电池容量和整体硬件配置（内存、存储、散热）经常被描述为“令人印象深刻”和“在这个价格下的超值表现”。
缺点： 软件更新： 这是最大的反复出现的问题之一（与之前的型号类似）；用户经常提到更新支持不稳定，担心长期的可靠性和安全性。 相机质量： 相机被视为主要的妥协点。 缺少的功能：常见投诉包括： 没有耳机插孔。 没有SD卡插槽。 缺乏IP68防水功能。 没有eSIM。

# Social Listening - Comments Analysis (KOLs)...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:132, y:3]**
Social Listening - Comments Analysis (KOLs)

**[position: x:9, y:50]**
Overall Sentiment:

- Tone: Positive - The audiences respond enthusiastically, highlighting the device’s performance, design, and value, while only occasionally noting minor trade-offs like camera quality or software updates.
Pros: 

- Performance & Specs:

  - The device is widely seen as powerful and more than capable for gaming and heavy use. 

    - Users highlight that it delivers “flagship-level performance at mid-range pricing.”

- Value for Money:

  - A major driver of positive sentiment.

    - Mny comments emphasize how impressive the specs are for the price, with comparisons to more expensive brands like Apple and Samsung.

- Display & Design:

  - The clean, full-screen display (no punch-hole) and overall aesthetics are highly praised. Users describe it as “super clean,” “modern,” and visually competitive with premium devices.

- Gaming Appeal:

  - Strong appeal among gamers looking for dedicated gaming devices. 

    - Some users explicitly mention buying or considering it as a gaming-focused secondary phone.

- Battery & Hardware Package:

  - Battery size and overall hardware package (RAM, storage, cooling) are frequently described as “impressive” and “over-delivering for the price.”
Cons:

- Software Updates:

  - One of the biggest recurring concerns (similar to previous models); users frequently mention inconsistent update support and worry about long-term reliability and security.

- Camera Quality:

  - Camera is seen as the main trade-off.

- Missing Features:

  - Common complaints include:

    - No headphone jack.

    - No SD card slot

    - Lack of IP68 water resistance

    - No eSIM

# Social listening (KOLs)...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:129, y:5]**
Social listening (KOLs)

**[position: x:14, y:41]**
- 整体情感： 语气：非常积极. 评论普遍将 REDMAGIC 11 Air 视为“超高性价比”设备，提供旗舰级性能却拥有中档价格。它经常被定位为一款强大的、以游戏为主的智能手机，且在其细分市场上几乎没有妥协。


- 赞扬与优点： 性能与游戏能力：始终被认为是其主要优势。Snapdragon 8 Elite 提供顶级的基准测试成绩和流畅的游戏体验（例如，在最高设置下稳定运行《原神》60fps）。 显示与沉浸感：因其超薄对称边框和通过屏下摄像头实现的无缝显示而备受赞赏。 强烈突出的特点。7,000 毫安时的电池提供了卓越的续航，80W 快充和充电绕过功能增强了长时间游戏的体验。 设计与构建质量：频繁被描述为高端且视觉上引人注目。透明元素和 RGB 灯效为其增添了独特的游戏身份感，而又不会显得过于夸张。 游戏功能与定制化：肩键和 Game Space UI 被视为有意义的补充，提供了高度可定制和优化的游戏体验。 性价比：是最强调的优点之一。被定位为“以更低的价格提供旗舰规格”。


- 关切与批评： 相机体验：通常被认为是一个弱点。虽然主摄像头尚可，但屏下前置摄像头拍摄的图像较为柔和，且质量有所限制。 连接性与端口：USB-C 2.0 被视为过时，评论员期望在这个级别提供更快的传输速度。 存储限制：没有 microSD 卡槽，限制了需要额外存储空间的用户的灵活性。 防水性：有限的保护（IP54）。开放式散热系统限制了达到更高水准防水的能力。 软件支持：虽然提到长期支持，但仍然存在关于更新一致性和推送速度的顾虑。 缺失功能：缺少耳机插孔和无线充电，尤其是与更高端的型号相比。

# Social listening (KOLs)...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:129, y:5]**
Social listening (KOLs)

**[position: x:14, y:41]**
- Overall Sentiment:

  - Tone: Very Positive – Reviewers widely highlight the REDMAGIC 11 Air as an “excellent value” device delivering flagship-level performance at a mid-range price. It is frequently positioned as a powerful, gaming-first smartphone with minimal compromises for its segment.

- Praise & Strengths: 

  - Performance & Gaming Power: Consistently praised as a key strength. The Snapdragon 8 Elite delivers top-tier benchmark results and smooth gameplay (e.g., Genshin Impact at stable 60fps on max settings).

  - Display & Immersion: Highly appreciated for its ultra-thin symmetrical bezels, and uninterrupted screen enabled by the under-display camera.

  - Strongly highlighted feature. The 7,000 mAh battery offers outstanding endurance, with 80W fast charging and charge bypass enhancing long gaming sessions.

  - Design & Build Quality: Frequently described as premium and visually striking. Transparent elements and RGB lighting add a distinctive gaming identity without feeling excessive.

  - Gaming Features & Customization: Shoulder triggers and Game Space UI are seen as meaningful additions, offering a highly customizable and optimized gaming experience.

  - Value for Money: One of the most emphasized strengths. Positioned as delivering “flagship specs for a lower price”

- Concerns & Critiques:

  - Camera Experience: Commonly noted as a weak point. While the main camera is acceptable, the under-display front camera produces softer images with visible quality limitations.

  - Connectivity & Port: USB-C 2.0 is viewed as outdated, with reviewers expecting faster transfer speeds at this level.

  - Storage Limitations: No microSD card slot, limiting flexibility for users needing additional storage.

  - Water Resistance: Limited protection (IP54). The open cooling system restricts the ability to achieve higher water resistance.

  - Software Support: While long-term support is mentioned, concerns remain regarding update consistency and rollout speed.

  - Missing Features: Absence of headphone jack and wireless charging noted, especially compared to higher-end models.

| Signature Transparent Design Reinforced Brand Identity | Non-Flat Back Design |
|  |  |
| - The transparent aesthetic was widely recognized as “core REDMAGIC DNA” | - 11 Pro’s (and 10 Series) flat back set a new design expectation among core users |
|  |  |
| - Compared to the 10 Air’s matte finish, the 11 Air felt more aligned with brand expectations | - 11 Air’s camera bump created inconsistency within the lineup |
|  |  |
| - Community perception: transparent design is now the default expectation, not a bonus | - Feedback indicates preference for a cleaner, fully flat design for aesthetics |
| Return of the Turbo Fan Improved Perceived Value | Removal of Headphone Jack |
|  |  |
| - Absence of a fan in the 10 Air created concerns around thermal performance | - Continues to be a recurring concern among core gamers |
|  |  |
| - Reintroduction in the 11 Air was positively received and seen as a correction | - Wired audio is still preferred for zero-latency gameplay |
|  |  |
| - Reinforced REDMAGIC’s positioning in active cooling leadership | - Seen as a compromise on competitive gaming experience |
| Strong Performance + Timing Advantage (Emulation Use Case) |  |
|  |  |
| - Performance met expectations for a Snapdragon 8 Elite device |  |
|  |  |
| - Launch timing aligned with new emulator driver releases, improving chip optimization, resulting in increased interest from emulation users |  |
|  |  |
| - Perceived as a more accessible alternative to the 11 Pro for high-performance gaming |  |
| Competitive Starting Price Expanded Accessibility |  |
|  |  |
| - Viewed as a strong entry point into mobile gaming |  |
|  |  |
| - Lower barrier to entry compared to flagship models |  |
|  |  |
| - Common use case: secondary device dedicated to gaming and emulation |  |

Social Listening (PR)

**[position: x:28, y:65]**
| Pros | Cons |
|---|---|
| 游戏性能：Snapdragon 8 Elite + RedCore R4 提供接近旗舰级的性能；能够平稳运行高帧率移动游戏和模拟器，极少发生降频。主动散热（风扇 + 蒸汽室 + 石墨烯层）确保长时间游戏时保持稳定性能。<br>显示屏：6.85 英寸 OLED 屏幕，1.5K 分辨率，144Hz 刷新率，高触控采样（最高 960–2000Hz），亮度（约 1800 尼特），屏下自拍相机提供沉浸式、不中断的观看体验。<br>设计：纤薄（约 7.85 毫米），轻便（约 207 克），舒适的弯曲背面，平衡长时间使用。流线型、透明/赛博朋克风格的外观，带有精致的 RGB 灯效。<br>电池与充电：大容量 7000 毫安时（部分地区为 7500 毫安时），支持全天高强度使用或 2 天以上的正常使用。80W 有线快充，采用充电分离技术以延长电池寿命；部分型号支持无线快充。<br>软件：简洁的 REDMAGIC OS 11（基于 Android 16），几乎无 bloatware，Game Space 中心提供 CPU/GPU 控制，可自定义肩部触发器（最高 520Hz），AI 游戏工具，战术教练，以及类似游戏机的叠加界面。<br>音频与触觉：双立体声扬声器，支持 DTS:X Ultra，高保真音效和精确的 X 轴或 4D 触觉反馈增强沉浸感。<br>价值主张：具有竞争力的中端价格（约 $499–599），提供旗舰级的游戏性能、沉浸式显示、大电池和先进散热技术，价格仅为顶级设备的部分成本。<br>额外优势：轻便的游戏手机形态。良好的构建质量，采用 Gorilla Glass 和铝合金框架，IP54 防尘防水。相较于前代 Air 模型，摄像头有所改进（50MP 主摄），适合日常拍照。 | 摄像头缺点：前置屏下自拍和超广角摄像头较弱；低光表现一般；不支持长焦或高级变焦。<br>充电与电池：不支持无线充电，仅支持有线充电；高瓦特数时充电稍慢。<br>散热与性能：主动风扇可能会有噪音；重度游戏时可能会有热降频；更薄的机身限制了持续的高峰性能。<br>设计与人体工学：专为玩家设计的外观（透明/RGB）可能并非所有人都喜欢；稍显笨重且滑手；背面面板容易留下指纹；仅有 IP54 防水/防尘保护。<br>软件与功能：RedMagic OS 功能较多，但比原生 Android 稍显粗糙；有轻微翻译和 bloatware 问题；软件更新支持较慢。<br>连接性与端口：没有耳机插孔、eSIM 或 microSD 扩展；仅支持 USB 2.0；与 Pro 版本相比，缺少一些高端配件。<br>音频：高音量时声音偏薄，缺乏深度；没有耳机插孔，需使用适配器。 |

Active cooling (fan + vapor chamber + graphene layers) maintains sustained performance during long sessions.<br><br>- Display: 6.85-inch OLED, 1.5K resolution, 144 Hz refresh rate, high touch sampling (up to 960–2000 Hz), bright (~1,800 nits), under-display selfie camera for immersive, uninterrupted viewing.<br><br>- Design: Slim (≈7.85 mm), lightweight (≈207 g), comfortable curved back, balanced for long sessions. Sleek, transparent/cyberpunk-inspired aesthetics with subtle RGB lighting.<br><br>- Battery & Charging: Large 7,000 mAh (some regions 7,500 mAh), supports full-day intensive use or 2+ days normal use. 80 W wired fast charging with charge separation for longevity; some models include wireless fast charging.<br><br>- Software: Clean REDMAGIC OS 11 on Android 16, minimal bloatware, Game Space hub with CPU/GPU control, customizable shoulder triggers (up to 520 Hz), AI gaming tools, Tactical Coach, and console-like overlays.<br><br>- Audio & Haptics: Dual stereo speakers, DTS:X Ultra support, high-fidelity sound, and precise X-axis or 4D haptic feedback enhance immersion.<br><br>- Value Proposition: Competitive mid-range price (~$499–599), delivering flagship-level gaming performance, immersive display, large battery, and advanced cooling at a fraction of the cost of top-tier devices.<br><br>- Additional Strengths: Lightweight form factor for a gaming phone. Good build quality with Gorilla Glass and aluminum frame, IP54 dust/water resistance. Camera improvements from previous Air models (50 MP main) suitable for casual photography. | - Camera Limitations: Front under-display selfie and ultra-wide cameras are weak; low-light performance mediocre; no telephoto or advanced zoom.<br><br>- Charging & Battery: No wireless charging; wired charging only; slightly slower charging at higher wattages. <br><br>- Cooling & Performance: Active fan can be noisy; thermal throttling under heavy gaming; slimmer body limits sustained peak performance.<br><br>- Design & Ergonomics: Gamer-focused design (transparent/RGB) may not appeal to all; slightly bulky and slippery; back panel attracts fingerprints; IP54 water/dust protection only. <br><br>- Software & Features: RedMagic OS functional but less polished than stock Android; minor translation and bloatware issues; slower software update support. <br><br>- Connectivity & Ports: No headphone jack, eSIM, or microSD expansion; USB 2.0 only; limited high-end extras compared to Pro variant. <br><br>- Audio: Sound can be thin or lacking depth at high volume; no wired audio option without adapter. |

# Pre sales - Customer Voice Listening Pre Sales Con...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:89, y:5]**
Pre sales - Customer Voice Listening Pre Sales Consultant in HTC

**[position: x:30, y:328]**
在launch阶段，客户的关注主要集中在产品的供应情况、产品细节、促销活动和政策问题上，显示出预售顾问主要在支持购买决策。
在早鸟优惠阶段，客户的关注转向了促销和支付问题，表明客户更关心如何顺利获取优惠，而不仅仅是了解产品。
一旦进入早期访问/开放销售阶段，客户的关注迅速转向订单修改和取消请求。在较长的开放销售期间，更多客户提出了信号/网络兼容性、取消、支付和退货相关的问题。
During the Launch stage, customer voice was mainly focused on availability, product details, promotions, and policy questions, showing that the pre-sale consultant was primarily supporting purchase decisions. In the Early Bird Offer stage, customer attention shifted more toward promotions and payment issues, indicating stronger interest in securing the offer smoothly rather than only understanding the product. Once Early Access / Open Sales began, customer voice moved quickly into order modification and cancellation requests. During the longer Open Sales period, more customers raised signal/network compatibility, cancellation, payment, and return-related concerns.

# After sales - Customer Voice Listening...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:93, y:5]**
After sales - Customer Voice Listening

**[position: x:548, y:18]**
Non -Tech Consultant in HTC

**[position: x:31, y:55]**
>> Top 10 Non-Tech Consultant

**[position: x:28, y:299]**
最大的关注点是取消订单、支付问题、退货请求、运输方式和订单修改，这意味着客户最关心的是结账后的订单取消灵活性和交货的确定性。
在早期访问阶段/开售初期，客户的声音迅速转向订单修改和取消订单，表明许多客户在下单后仍希望调整购买。
在较长的开售期间，客户的关注点转向改变主意、要求在发货前取消订单、运输和交货时间、以及风险控制/支付清算等问题。
总体来说，非技术类的售后数据表明，客户并不主要要求复杂的服务处理，而是表达了对购后灵活性、顺畅的支付处理和明确的交货预期的强烈需求。

The biggest concerns were cancellation, payment issues, return requests, shipping method, and order modification, which means customers cared most about order flexibility and delivery certainty after checkout.
In the Early Access / first days of Open Sales, the voice shifted especially quickly toward modify order and cancel order, showing that many customers still wanted to adjust their purchase right after ordering.
During the longer Open Sales period, changed mind, request cancel before shipment, shipping & delivery time, and risk-control/payment clearance became the most visible detailed concerns.
Overall, the non-tech after-sales data shows that customers were not mainly asking for complex service handling, but were instead expressing a strong need for post-purchase flexibility, smooth payment processing, and clear delivery expectations.

  file_url: "https://docs.google.com/presentation/d/1dYAL0iODb1x0sJpOQqMhcl0c5hni5OrPN-jl0-3K2x0/edit#slide=id.g3b6aa73c387_7_40"

# After sales - Customer Voice Listening...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: y:4]**
| After sales - Customer Voice Listening | Tech Consultant in HTC |
|---|---|

**[position: x:30, y:315]**
整体而言，技术类售后工作量相对较小，但在开售期间变得更加突出。
客户的关注主要集中在信号和网络相关的问题，特别是运营商不支持、无信号和网络兼容性查询。
这表明，主要的技术问题不是广泛的硬件故障，而是产品是否能与客户所在地区的运营商和网络环境良好兼容。
与早期阶段相比，启动和早鸟阶段的技术问题较少，但随着更多客户开始使用产品，问题在开售阶段有所增加。
总体而言，售后技术数据表明，网络兼容性期望管理是本次活动中最关键的技术客户声音主题。

The technical after-sales workload was relatively small overall, but it became much more visible during the Open Sales period.
The customer voice was highly concentrated in signal and network-related issues, especially carrier not supported, no signal, and network compatibility inquiries.
This shows that the main technical concern was not broad hardware failure, but whether the product could actually work well with the customer’s local carrier and network environment.
Compared with earlier stages, technical issues were limited during Launch and Early Bird, but increased after more customers started using the product during Open Sales.
Overall, the after-sales tech data suggests that network compatibility expectation management was the key technical customer voice theme in this campaign.


=====

# After sales - Customer Voice Listening...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:90, y:4]**
After sales - Customer Voice Listening

**[position: x:546, y:17]**
Return Reason HTC退货

**[position: x:24, y:61]**
退货主要由意愿改变和兼容性问题驱动，而非真正的产品缺陷。（55个退货案例）

- 改变主意/偏好：22个案例（40.0%）

- 网络/运营商不兼容：19个案例（34.5%）

- 特性/兼容性差距：5个案例（9.1%）

- 产品问题/性能：5个案例（9.1%）

- 维修案例：3个案例（5.5%）

- 用户适配/舒适度：1个案例（1.8%）
退货数据表明，主要问题不是硬件质量的普遍问题，而是客户期望与实际不符、运营商兼容性不匹配以及购买后改变主意的问题。
根据退货数据，客户最关心的是产品是否能够在实际使用场景中顺利工作，特别是在与当地运营商和网络的兼容性方面。
此外，一些客户明确希望支持特定的功能，如eSIM、显示输出或应用兼容性，这意味着他们的购买决策更多地与实际使用案例相关，而不仅仅是对产品的兴趣。


=====

  file_url: "https://docs.google.com/presentation/d/1dYAL0iODb1x0sJpOQqMhcl0c5hni5OrPN-jl0-3K2x0/edit#slide=id.g3b6aa73c387_7_52"
  title: "[REDMAGIC] RM 11 Air Launch report"

# After sales - Customer Voice Listening...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:90, y:4]**
After sales - Customer Voice Listening

**[position: x:546, y:17]**
Return Reason HTC退货

**[position: x:24, y:61]**
The clearest finding is that the return pool is driven much more by commercial / compatibility reasons than by true product defects. ( 55 Return Case)

- Changed mind / preference: 22 cases (40.0%)

- Network / carrier incompatibility: 19 cases (34.5%)

- Feature / compatibility gap: 5 cases (9.1%)

- Product issue / performance: 5 cases (9.1%)

- Repair cases: 3 cases (5.5%)

- User fit / comfort: 1 case (1.8%)
The return data suggests the main issue is not broad hardware quality, but rather customer expectation mismatch, carrier compatibility mismatch, and post-purchase change of mind.
Based on the return data, customers cared most about whether the product could work smoothly in their real-life usage scenario, especially with their local carrier and network. 
In addition, some customers clearly expected support for specific features such as eSIM, dual SIM, display output, or app compatibility, which means their buying decision was closely tied to practical use cases rather than only general product interest.

# Future Improvements...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:93, y:3]**
Future Improvements

**[position: x:24, y:62]**
定位与增长：
REDMAGIC 11 Air 已被市场认可为一款具有战略意义的中端入门产品，相较于前代产品，提供了更明确的产品定义和更具竞争力的定价。长期的成功依赖于我们能够积极扩大新客户的获取。
增加对高表现市场的关注：
美国与欧洲（EU）：这两个地区是销售的核心驱动因素。美国继续创造显著的收入，占总销售额的近40%，而欧洲，尤其是法国，是一个重要的增长市场。墨西哥与巴西：这些拉美市场表现出强劲的增长和提高的转化率。继续通过注重价值的促销和产品 / 营销本地化推动需求。
生命周期与活动管理：
流量溢出规划：在5月和6月，利用即将推出的Astra 2和11S Pro等产品的流量，交叉推广11 Air作为中端替代品。季度规划：为Q4准备专门的活动，利用GamesCom和黑五等零售高峰期进行促销。
KOL策略：
通过高效的合作形式，如联合赠品和挑战等，来多样化KOL合作，以缓解内容疲劳并最大化创作者的公信力。

# Future Improvements...

## 🖼️ Elements Position
**[position: x:93, y:3]**
Future Improvements

**[position: x:25, y:67]**
Positioning & Growth: The REDMAGIC 11 Air is established as a strategic mid-range entry, offering more distinct product definition and competitive pricing than its predecessor. Long-term success lies on our ability to scale new customer acquisition.
Increase Focus on High-Performing Markets:

- United States & Europe (EU): Both regions are core drivers of sales. The U.S. continues to generate significant revenue, contributing nearly 40% of total sales, while Europe, led by France, is an important growing market.

- Mexico & Brazil: These Latin American markets have shown strong growth and increased conversion rates. Continue driving demand through value-sensitive promotions and product localization. 
Lifecycle & Campaign Management

- Traffic Spillover Planning: In May and June, leverage the traffic from upcoming launches like Astra 2 and 11S Pro to cross-promote the 11 Air as a mid-range alternative.

- Quarterly Planning: Prepare dedicated activations for GamesCom and Black Friday in Q4 to capitalize on peak retail periods.
Influencer Strategy: 
Diversify KOL partnerships through high-utility formats, such as joint giveaways and challenges, to mitigate content fatigue and maximize creator credibility.


=====
